*Muhammad Ashad Kabir,Md Ahshanul Haque*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究评估了在零样本设置下使用预训练大语言模型（GPT-4o-mini）基于孟加拉国人口健康调查数据预测儿童发育迟缓的可行性、公平性和时间稳健性，结果显示其平衡准确率与监督式随机森林基线相当，敏感性更高，但在居住地和家庭财富类别间存在公平性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 儿童营养不良在中低收入国家（尤其是南亚）仍是重大公共卫生挑战，早期识别易受影响儿童对及时干预和资源分配至关重要。需要评估基础模型在公共健康预测场景中的实际应用潜力。

Method: 使用2007至2022年孟加拉国人口健康调查（BDHS）数据，将孕产妇、儿童、医疗保健和家庭特征转化为基于提示的语义可解释表示，采用GPT-4o-mini进行零样本发育迟缓预测，并与随机森林基线对比，评估人口和社会经济群体的公平性及不同调查波次间的时间稳健性。

Result: GPT-4o-mini零样本推理实现了与监督基线相当的平衡准确率，同时识别发育迟缓病例的敏感性显著更高；在儿童性别群体间表现相对一致；跨BDHS调查波次预测行为稳定；但在居住地和家庭财富类别间观察到重要的公平性差异。

Conclusion: 大型语言模型在零样本条件下可作为一种有前景的工具用于儿童发育迟缓筛查，但在部署于公共卫生预测场景之前，需要进一步研究解决居住地和家庭财富方面的公平性差距。

Abstract: Child malnutrition remains a major public health challenge in low- and middle-income countries, particularly in South Asia, where early identification of vulnerable children is critical for timely intervention and resource allocation. This study aims to evaluate the feasibility, fairness, and temporal robustness of using a pretrained large language model (LLM) in a zero-shot setting for child stunting prediction using population health survey data. Using Bangladesh Demographic and Health Survey (BDHS) data collected between 2007 and 2022, we transformed maternal, child, healthcare, and household characteristics into semantically interpretable prompt-based representations and evaluated GPT-4o-mini for zero-shot stunting prediction, comparing its performance against a random forest baseline and assessing fairness across demographic and socioeconomic groups as well as temporal robustness across survey waves. The results demonstrate that zero-shot inference using GPT-4o-mini achieved comparable balanced accuracy to the supervised baseline while exhibiting substantially higher sensitivity for identifying stunting cases, relatively consistent performance across child sex groups, and stable predictive behaviour across BDHS waves; however, important fairness disparities were observed across residence and household wealth categories, highlighting the need for further investigation before deployment of foundation models in public health prediction settings.

</details>


### [30] [M3-DuplexBench: A Multi-Turn, Multilingual, Multidomain Benchmark for Full-Duplex Spoken Dialogue Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29125)
*Ryo Fukuda,Atsushi Ando,Hiroki Kanagawa,Takatomo Kano,Marc Delcroix,Naohiro Tawara,Yuya Chiba*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出M3-DuplexBench，一个面向全双工口语对话系统（FDSDSs）的多轮、多语言、多领域基准测试集，支持英语和日语，涵盖日常对话和多轮问答，并在多种对话上下文设置下评估模型以分析对话历史的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全双工口语对话系统虽可实现边听边说、平滑轮转、回话处理和用户打断处理等自然行为，但现有基准在多轮对话中的公平比较存在困难，且语言和对话领域覆盖有限。因此需要一个新的基准来解决这些局限。

Method: 提出M3-DuplexBench基准，支持英语和日语，覆盖日常对话与多轮问答两个领域。在多种对话上下文设置下评估模型，包括单轮、仅用户侧（user-only）以及教师强制的全上下文（teacher-forced full-context）设置，以分析对话历史对模型行为的影响。

Result: 对近期FDSDS模型的实验揭示了模型特有的轮转特性、跨语言和跨领域的明显性能差距，以及对话上下文带来的混合影响。

Conclusion: M3-DuplexBench为FDSDSs提供了更全面的多轮、多语言、多领域评估框架，有助于公平比较并深入理解对话历史对全双工系统行为的影响。

Abstract: Full-duplex spoken dialogue systems (FDSDSs) can listen while speaking, enabling natural behaviors such as smooth turn-taking, backchannel handling, and user barge-in handling. However, fair comparisons in multi-turn conversations remain a challenge. In addition, existing benchmarks provide limited coverage of languages and dialogue domains. We propose M3-DuplexBench, a multi-turn, multilingual, multidomain benchmark for FDSDSs. M3-DuplexBench supports English and Japanese and covers both casual conversation and multi-turn question answering. In addition, we evaluate models under multiple dialogue context settings, including single-turn, user-only, and teacher-forced full-context settings, to analyze how dialogue history affects model behavior. Experiments with recent FDSDSs reveal model-specific turn-taking characteristics, clear performance gaps across languages and domains, and mixed effects of dialogue context.

</details>


### [31] [Authorship Verification of Transcribed German-Language Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29168)
*Oren Halvani,Sophie Titze*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次将作者验证（AV）应用于德语视频转录文本，使用10种AV方法在自建的3个语料库（150位说话人的300个视频）上进行实验，发现基于字符和词n-gram的传统AV方法表现最佳（准确率达88%，AUC达90%），而现代基于Transformer的方法在所有语料上表现显著较差，表明传统方法在该领域仍然具有竞争力和相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者验证领域虽已取得显著进展，但仍存在两个重要研究空白：一是绝大多数AV研究聚焦于书面文本，忽视了口语表达形式（如视频中的语音）；二是现有研究主要集中在英语，德语等其他语言受到的关注相对较少。为填补这些空白，本文探索将AV方法应用于德语视频转录文本这一新场景。

Method: 文章将10种AV方法（涵盖传统方法和现代Transformer方法）应用于德语视频转录文本，验证说话人在视频对中的身份一致性。作者自建了包含300个视频、150位说话人的三个语料库，并在此上进行系统的实验评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，基于简单字符n-gram和词n-gram表示的传统AV方法表现最佳，最优准确率可达88%，AUC可达90%。相比之下，更现代的Transformer方法在所有评估语料上表现显著较差。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，在德语口语视频转录文本的作者验证任务中，传统AV方法仍然保持竞争力和相关性，甚至胜过更现代的Transformer方法，这为AV领域的研究方向提供了重要启示。

Abstract: Authorship Verification (AV) represents an important subfield of digital text forensics and addresses the fundamental question of whether two texts were written by the same author. Although the field has made substantial progress over the past two decades, several important challenges remain unresolved or underexplored. For instance, most AV research has focused on written texts, despite the fact that language is expressed not only in written but also in spoken form, such as in videos. Moreover, existing AV studies have predominantly concentrated on English, while other languages, including German, have received comparatively little attention. To address these research gaps, we apply AV to spoken language in the form of transcripts of German-language videos and examine the effectiveness of established AV methods in verifying a speaker's identity across video pairs. Our experimental evaluation, based on a total of ten AV methods applied to three self-compiled corpora comprising 300 videos from 150 speakers, shows that the best performance (up to 88% accuracy and 90% AUC) is achieved by traditional AV approaches based on simple character- and token n-gram representations. In contrast, more modern transformer-based approaches perform significantly worse on all evaluated corpora. Our results therefore suggest that traditional methods in the field of AV remain both competitive and relevant.

</details>


### [32] [Learning Latent Reasoning Traces for Scalar Reward Models End-to-End](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29185)
*Sanwoo Lee,Clive Bai,Hsiu-Yuan Huang,Kun Liang,Weijie Liu,Yunfang Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LatentRM，一种将中间推理轨迹建模为离散潜在变量，以显式最大化下游标量奖励似然的奖励建模框架。通过端到端的on-policy优化，LatentRM将深度推理评估与精确评分紧密结合，在分布内/分布外数据集及RLHF中均优于标量、生成式和混合奖励模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统标量奖励模型（scalar RMs）虽然高效且具有概率可解释性，但依赖表面线索，难以泛化到复杂或分布外（OOD）任务；生成式奖励模型（generative RMs）利用深度推理提升鲁棒性，但其自然语言形式的分数缺乏标量模型的数值灵活性和概率可解释性。现有混合方法通过off-policy多任务学习并行结合两者，但无法保证生成的推理轨迹真正服务于下游标量奖励预测，二者之间存在"错配"问题。

Method: LatentRM将中间推理轨迹视为离散潜在变量，通过on-policy策略端到端优化潜在推理空间，显式最大化下游标量奖励的似然函数。该方法将推理评估与精确打分紧密耦合，克服了off-policy并行优化无法保证推理轨迹与标量预测对齐的缺陷。

Result: 在分布内和分布外数据集以及RLHF场景下的大量验证表明，LatentRM在偏好建模和策略对齐方面优于标量、生成式和混合奖励模型，涵盖从开放式对话到复杂推理的多种任务。

Conclusion: LatentRM通过将推理过程与标量奖励预测在潜在空间中端到端联合优化，成功融合了生成式推理的鲁棒性与标量建模的可解释性，为奖励建模提供了更优的范式。

Abstract: Reward models (RMs) are central to aligning large language models with human preferences via reinforcement learning. Although traditional scalar RMs enable efficient and probabilistic reward modeling, they rely on superficial cues that fail to generalize to complex or out-of-distribution (OOD) tasks. Conversely, generative RMs leverage extensive reasoning to improve robustness on challenging tasks, but their natural language-based scores lack the numerical flexibility and probabilistic interpretability that scalar RMs offer. While recent approaches combine both paradigms through off-policy multi-task learning, such parallel optimization does not guarantee that generated reasoning traces actively align with or benefit downstream scalar reward prediction. To address this mismatch, we propose LatentRM, a reward modeling framework that learns intermediate reasoning traces as discrete latent variables to explicitly maximize the likelihood of downstream scalar rewards. Through on-policy optimization of the latent reasoning space end-to-end, LatentRM tightly couples deep reasoning-based evaluation with precise scoring. Extensive validations on in-distribution and OOD datasets and RLHF show that LatentRM outperforms scalar, generative, and hybrid RMs on preference modeling and policy alignment across tasks ranging from open-ended conversation to complex reasoning.

</details>


### [33] [Detecting Experiential Intertextuality Across Migration Routes: Beyond Surface Similarity in French Narratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29188)
*Sakayo Toadoum Sari,Nelly Robin,Michelle Auzanneau,Lakhdar Sais,Veronique Petit,Marie Veniard,Said Jabbour,Fabien Delorme*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了"经验性互文性检测"这一新任务，即在不依赖标注训练数据的情况下，自动识别不同迁移叙事（如跨撒哈拉和巴尔干走廊）中共享的相似生活经验。作者从108篇法语迁移叙事中自动生成句子对，使用多种无标注方法评估（词汇基线、句嵌入、POS结构特征、迁移主题词典、叙事感知特征及零样本LLM打分），并与816条专家标注的互文性判断进行验证。结果显示零样本Qwen2.5-7B表现最佳（r=0.38），而融合全部31个特征的监督混合方法达到r=0.45，相比最优单方法提升21%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迁移者在不同地理路线上（如跨撒哈拉与巴尔干走廊）的叙事中常出现惊人相似的生活经验——警察暴力、走私者剥削、危险穿越和家庭分离。作者提出"经验性互文性检测"任务，旨在自动识别这些跨越叙事的共享经验回响，以支持对迁移叙事的比较文学与计算人文研究，且不需要昂贵的标注训练数据。

Method: 从108篇法语迁移叙事中自动生成句子对，采用多种无标注方法进行评分：词汇基线、句子嵌入、基于POS的结构特征、迁移特定主题词典、上下文感知叙事特征，以及两种零样本LLM（Qwen2.5-7B和Mistral-7B）下的三种提示策略。此外还构建了融合全部31个特征的监督混合模型。所有方法均与816条专家标注的互文性判断（标注者间Krippendorff's α=0.27）进行相关性验证。

Result: 所有表层、结构和嵌入方法仅与专家判断弱相关（r≤0.30）；Qwen2.5-7B零样本打出最佳单一方法相关度（r=0.38）；少样本示例降低Qwen性能但显著提升Mistral；叙事位置显著预测互文性，出发阶段句子对的经验回响最强；融合31个特征的监督混合模型达到r=0.45，较最佳单一方法提升21%。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了经验性互文性检测的挑战性：传统表层与嵌入方法难以捕捉深层经验共鸣，而大型语言模型（尤其Qwen2.5-7B）展现出更强的能力。叙事位置作为重要信号表明结构信息对理解迁移叙事互文性的价值。监督融合模型虽为最佳，但无标注的LLM方法已具备可行竞争力，为无需专家标注的迁移叙事比较分析提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Migrants traversing geographically distinct routes such as the Trans-Saharan and Balkan corridors often recount strikingly parallel lived experiences: police violence, smuggler exploitation, dangerous crossings, and family separation. We introduce the task of experiential intertextuality detection: automatically identifying shared experiential echoes across migration narratives without requiring annotated training data. From 108 French migration narratives spanning both corridors, we automatically generate sentence pairs and score them using annotation-free methods: lexical baselines, sentence embeddings, POS-based structural features, a migration-specific theme lexicon, context-aware narrative features, and zero-shot LLM scoring with Qwen2.5-7B and Mistral-7B under three prompting strategies. We validate all methods against 816 expert-annotated intertextuality judgments (inter-annotator Krippendorff's $α= 0.27$). Our results reveal that all surface, structural, and embedding methods correlate only weakly with expert judgments ($r \leq 0.30$); Qwen2.5-7B zero-shot achieves the best single-method correlation ($r = 0.38$); few-shot examples degrade Qwen but dramatically improve Mistral; narrative position significantly predicts intertextuality, with departure-phase pairs showing the highest experiential echoes; and a supervised hybrid combining all 31 features achieves $r = 0.45$, a 21% improvement over the best individual method.

</details>


### [34] [Hy-MultiTurn: A Six-Dimensional Benchmark for Deep Multi-Turn Dialogue Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29196)
*Eileen Ye,Jiawen Tao,Yaoming Li,Chenxu Liu,Wenhan Yu,Yaxin Fan,Xiaokun Yuan,Mengzhou Wu,Yanbing Jiang,Maxm Pan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Hy-MultiTurn是一个用于深度多轮对话理解的中文基准测试，基于真实聊天机器人失败案例归纳出六种失效机制，构建了209个跨12-76轮的控制任务，涵盖约束记忆、精确执行、约束综合、对象定位、行动抑制和指代消解六个模式。评估22个前沿模型配置后发现即使最强的GPT-5.5也只能在41.1%的响应中满足全部要求，且没有模型在所有六个模式中表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多轮对话基准通常只覆盖短对话，未充分评估模型在长多轮交互中的能力（尤其是中文场景），且对模型的失败机制缺乏深入洞察。真实场景中长对话要求模型记住早期细节、追踪后续修订、识别指代对象并在条件未满足时抑制行动，现有基准无法满足这些评测需求。

Method: 首先分析真实聊天机器人的失败案例，归纳出六种重复出现的失效机制；基于这些机制定义六种受控评测模式：约束记忆、精确执行、约束综合、对象定位、行动抑制和指代消解；构建209个受控任务，对话长度为12-76轮，并通过对话长度、无关话题干扰和口语化表达增加难度。

Result: 对22个前沿模型配置的评估显示Hy-MultiTurn具有广泛挑战性：最强的整体配置GPT-5.5在所有响应中仅有41.1%满足全部要求，且没有任何模型在所有六个模式中均表现最佳。

Conclusion: Hy-MultiTurn填补了中文长多轮对话深度理解评测的空白，验证了现有前沿模型在该任务上的显著不足，为后续模型改进提供了细粒度的能力诊断方向。

Abstract: Long-running multi-turn interactions with chatbots and agents are now common, and a correct response often depends on remembering earlier details, tracking later revisions, identifying intended objects or referents, and withholding action when required conditions are unmet. Existing multi-turn benchmarks typically cover short exchanges and do not fully evaluate these capabilities in long multi-turn interactions, particularly in Chinese, while offering limited insight into how and why models fail. To address these limitations, we analyze real chatbot failures to identify six recurring mechanisms and use them to define six controlled evaluation modes in Hy-MultiTurn, a Chinese benchmark for deep multi-turn dialogue understanding. The six modes evaluate constraint memory, precise execution, constraint synthesis, object localization, action suppression, and reference resolution. Across the six modes, we construct 209 controlled tasks spanning 12-76 turns, with dialogue length, irrelevant-topic distraction, and colloquial phrasing adding further difficulty. Evaluation of 22 frontier model configurations shows that Hy-MultiTurn is broadly challenging, as even GPT-5.5, the strongest overall configuration, satisfies all requirements in only 41.1 percent of responses and no model performs best in all six modes.

### [35] [Knowing When to Quit: Diagnosing and Training LLMs to Abort Futile Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29211)
*Xinyan Guan,Jiali Zeng,Chunlei Xin,Yaojie Lu,Hongyu Lin,Xianpei Han,Le Sun,Fandong Meng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统性地刻画了大语言模型在超出其能力范围的任务上产生"徒劳推理"（futile reasoning）的现象，即生成计算昂贵但语义空洞的推理内容。作者提出CaRL（能力对齐强化学习）方法，通过奖励塑形和事后拒绝增强来对齐模型行为与能力边界，在显著减少徒劳推理的同时保持任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在超出其能力范围的任务上会产生看似合理但包含细微错误的推理，这类"徒劳推理"不仅计算开销大，且容易误导用户。现有研究缺乏对该现象的刻画以及有效的解决方案。

Method: CaRL方法包含两个关键组件：(1) 奖励塑形——通过对拒绝行为给予激励、对徒劳推理给予惩罚的方式来引导模型行为与能力边界对齐；(2) 事后拒绝增强——将失败的推理尝试转化为拒绝监督信号，扩展训练数据。

Result: 实验表明CaRL在各类任务难度下均能大幅减少徒劳推理，同时保持原有任务性能，实现了能力对齐行为而不牺牲实用性。

Conclusion: 大语言模型存在系统性的能力过冲和"能力-行为"之间校准不当的问题，占主导地位的失败模式是看似有效但暗含细微错误的"貌似有理的推理"（specious reasoning），且随任务难度增加而加剧。CaRL为这一挑战提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models generate computationally expensive yet semantically void reasoning on beyond-capability tasks, creating risks where plausible-sounding but incorrect derivations mislead users. We characterize this \textit{futile reasoning} phenomenon through systematic analysis, revealing universal capability overreach and systematic miscalibration between capability and behavior. The dominant failure mode is specious reasoning, which outputs look superficially valid but contain subtle errors, escalating with task difficulty. To address this, we introduce \textbf{CaRL} (\textbf{Ca}pability-\textbf{a}ligned \textbf{R}einforcement \textbf{L}earning), which aligns model behavior with capability boundaries through reward shaping that incentivizes refusal over futile reasoning and hindsight refusal augmentation that converts failures into refusal supervision. Experiments demonstrate a substantial reduction in futile reasoning while preserving performance across task difficulties, effectively achieving capability-aligned behavior without sacrificing utility. \footnote{https://github.com/icip-cas/Knowing-When-to-Quit}

</details>

*Antorweep Chakravorty*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: InMyStyle是一个隐私优先的单用户系统，利用多个本地辅助LLM构建配对训练数据，将小语言模型微调为LoRA适配器，将AI编辑文本改写为用户个人写作风格，无需推理时的指令提示。在219个科学论文语料对中，各模型规模（0.5B-7B）的自动综合得分均稳定在0.69，表明小模型足以胜任该改写任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI文本编辑往往带有通用化的"AI味"且缺乏个性化，用户希望AI改写结果能贴合个人写作风格。同时，隐私保护要求使得无法依赖云端大模型处理用户私人文档，因此需要一种本地化、隐私优先且无需指令提示即可适应个人写作风格的系统。

Method: 该系统利用用户提供的历史文档，通过多个本地辅助LLM构造配对训练样本（AI编辑前后文本对），在0.5B到7B参数范围的基座模型上微调LoRA适配器。推理时无需指令提示，直接进行风格改写。此外设计了长度感知的生成预算和自动分块机制以支持不同长度的输入。

Result: 在219个科学论文语料评估对上，所有模型规模在贪心解码和采样解码下自动综合得分均稳定在0.69；400条来自五个LLM评委的评分显示，InMyStyle输出的平均感知AI-ness得分比辅助AI生成的输入低20%以上，且感知AI-ness得分随模型规模减小而降低。

Conclusion: 结果表明小模型足以完成该风格改写任务，模型大小主要影响性能权衡而非稳定质量排序。系统能显著降低AI生成文本的"AI味"，实现个性化风格改写，同时保持隐私优先的设计原则。

Abstract: InMyStyle is a privacy first, single user system that adapts small language models to rewrite AI-edited text towards an individual user's writing style without an instruction prompt at inference. Given a user's documents, it uses multiple local helper LLMs to construct paired training examples and fine tunes LoRA adapters on base models ranging from 0.5B to 7B parameters. Length aware generation budgets and automatic chunking support inputs of different lengths. On 219 evaluation pairs from a scientific-paper corpus, the automatic composite score plateaus at 0.69 [scale 0-1] across all model sizes under both greedy and sampled decoding. This observed plateau suggests that small models are sufficient for the measured rewriting task, with model size determining trade-offs rather than a stable quality ranking. As a secondary evaluation, 400 ratings from five LLM judges give InMyStyle outputs a mean perceived AI-ness score over 20% lower than their helper-AI generated inputs, while mean perceived AI-ness scores decrease with model size within InMyStyle.

</details>


### [37] [Data Turnstile: A Scalable Open Framework for Function-Calling Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29250)
*Goutham Ramakrishnan,Megha Sharma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Data Turnstile，一个开源框架，通过按步骤分解多轮工具使用交互并引入验证和错误反馈循环，从用户定义的API规范生成高质量合成函数调用训练数据，使小型语言模型在工具使用任务上显著提升性能，接近甚至超越远大于自身规模的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型语言模型（SLM）因低延迟、低成本和隐私优势适合智能体部署，但在工具使用任务上表现不佳，因为训练数据稀缺且存在噪声。与大型模型不同，SLM无法通过容量弥补低质量监督，数据质量成为关键瓶颈。因此需要一种能生成高质量合成训练数据的方法来解决SLM在函数调用任务上的数据困境。

Method: Data Turnstile框架将多轮工具使用交互分解为受限的逐步生成流程，包含验证和错误反馈循环。该方法可从用户定义的API规范出发，精细控制API多样性、对话复杂度和输出正确性，从而生成高质量合成训练数据。

Result: 在BFCL单轮基准上，用Turnstile数据微调的Qwen3-0.6B（无思维链）达到75.9%准确率，优于启用思维链的基座67.4%，并接近Qwen3-1.7B（78.4%）和Qwen3-4B（79.9%），尽管分别小3倍和7倍。在τ²-bench多轮智能体基准上，Turnstile训练的Qwen3-1.7B在Telecom领域达到31.1% pass^1，比基座6.6%提升4.7倍，超过Qwen2.5-32B-Instruct（27.4%）；Qwen3-0.6B达24.6%，比基座3.5%提升7倍，接近32B模型。同时发布了包含1000+ API和10万+多轮交互的数据集。

Conclusion: Data Turnstile通过可控的分解式合成数据生成，有效解决了SLM在工具使用任务中的数据瓶颈问题，使小模型在函数调用基准上获得大幅提升，甚至超越大模型，验证了高质量合成数据对SLM能力提升的关键作用。

Abstract: Small language models (SLMs) are attractive for agentic deployment due to low latency, reduced cost, and on-device privacy, yet they struggle with tool-use tasks where training data is scarce and noisy. Unlike larger models, SLMs cannot compensate for low-quality supervision through sheer capacity, making data quality the critical bottleneck. We present Data Turnstile, an open-source framework that takes user-defined API specifications and generates high-quality synthetic training data for function calling. Turnstile decomposes multi-turn tool-use interactions into constrained, stepwise generation with validation and error-feedback loops, providing fine-grained control over API diversity, conversation complexity, and output correctness. We demonstrate effectiveness of domain adaptation with Turnstile data on two challenging function calling benchmarks. On the BFCL single-turn benchmark, a Qwen3-0.6B fine-tuned on Turnstile data without chain-of-thought achieves 75.9% overall accuracy (versus 67.4% for the base model with thinking enabled), closing the gap with thinking-enabled Qwen3-1.7B (78.4%) and Qwen3-4B (79.9%) despite being 3$\times$ and 7$\times$ smaller respectively. On $τ^2$-bench, a multi-turn agentic benchmark, Turnstile-trained Qwen3-1.7B achieves 31.1% pass^1 on the Telecom domain, improving 4.7$\times$ over its 6.6% base and surpassing Qwen2.5-32B-Instruct (27.4%), a model 19$\times$ larger. Turnstile-trained Qwen3-0.6B achieves 24.6%, improving 7$\times$ over its 3.5% base and approaching the 32B model (53$\times$ larger). We release Data Turnstile along with a dataset spanning 1,000+ APIs and 100K+ multi-turn interactions.

</details>

*Mengting Chen,Yanshu Sun,Wanting Liang,Beidi Luan,Rui Sun,Dezhi Chen,Jing Li,Zuo Bai*

Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Reliable evaluation of open-ended LLM outputs requires fine-grained rubrics, yet expert curation is costly and difficult to scale. Existing automated pipelines rely on strict judge unanimity and binary variance filters, which cannot distinguish measurable rubrics from informative ones. We introduce CalibratedRubric, a task-adaptive framework that combines type-specific scoring, Bayesian rubric-measurability filtering, and item response theory (IRT)-based bank assembly. CalibratedRubric estimates each rubric's measurability with a Beta--Bernoulli agreement posterior and uses a submodular information-coverage objective to construct compact rubric banks over the observed capability range. Across financial, healthcare, general, and legal benchmarks, measurability filtering improves human-gold agreement on JudgmentBench from $κ=0.604$ to $0.743$. IRT-based greedy selection improves cross-fitted rank fidelity over random selection across all six evaluated response blocks and requires only 49 rather than 131 rubrics to reach the target correlation on FinResearchBench decision-support tasks. Task-label perturbations further reduce system separation, confirming the practical relevance of task-adaptive scoring. These results support CalibratedRubric as an efficient, uncertainty-aware approach to open-ended LLM evaluation, with calibration gains depending on sufficient judge redundancy.

</details>


### [39] [Translation with Thought: Difficulty-Adaptive Reasoning via Reinforcement Learning for Multi-Domain Machine Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29287)
*Yongshi Ye,Biao Fu,Chongxuan Huang,Yidong Chen,Xiaodong Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TwT（Translation with Thought），一个资源理性框架，通过自适应地在直觉与深思推理之间切换来优化多领域机器翻译（MDMT）。TwT两阶段训练：先在有难度感知的长链思维轨迹上监督微调，再用混合奖励的强化学习优化翻译质量和推理效率。在15个基准、3种可见语言和59种未见语言上，TwT-7B和TwT-14B以32-60%的token节省超越更大规模SOTA推理模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多领域机器翻译面临不同领域间语言复杂度差异的挑战。现有模型采用统一的推理策略，无法根据翻译难度自适应调整推理努力，导致简单翻译浪费计算资源，复杂翻译推理不足。作者从人类译员根据难度调整推理努力这一认知机制中获得启发，旨在构建将认知原则融入翻译行为的资源理性框架。

Method: TwT采用两阶段训练：第一阶段，从DeepSeek-R1蒸馏具备难度感知的长链思维（CoT）轨迹，并用GPT-4o改写以反映人类式推理经济性（即避免不必要的冗长思考），在此基础上进行监督微调；第二阶段，使用结合翻译质量和推理效率的混合奖励信号进行强化学习，使模型学会在简单任务上快速响应（直觉推理）而在复杂任务上深入思考（深思推理），实现推理努力的自适应调节。

Result: 在15个基准（涵盖域内和域外）、3种可见语言和59种未见语言上评估，并在三种骨干模型上进行消融实验。TwT-7B和TwT-14B在翻译质量上超越更大规模的SOTA推理模型，同时将token使用量减少32-60%。

Conclusion: 将翻译行为与认知原则对齐能够实现MDMT中的鲁棒泛化、高质量翻译和高效推理。这表明资源理性推理策略不仅有效，且具有跨语言、跨领域的泛化能力，为高效推理模型提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Multi-domain machine translation (MDMT) poses a unique challenge due to varying levels of linguistic complexity across domains. Inspired by human translators' ability to adapt reasoning effort based on difficulty, we propose TwT (Translation with Thought), a resource-rational framework that learns to modulate inference between intuitive and deliberate reasoning. TwT is trained in two stages: (1) supervised fine-tuning on difficulty-aware long chain-of-thought traces distilled from DeepSeek-R1 and rewritten by GPT-4o to reflect human-like reasoning economy, and (2) reinforcement learning with a hybrid reward to optimize translation quality and reasoning efficiency. Evaluated on 15 benchmarks spanning in-domain and out-of-domain settings, as well as 3 seen and 59 unseen languages, with ablations across three backbone models, TwT-7B and TwT-14B outperform much larger SOTA reasoning models in translation quality, while reducing token usage by 32--60\%. These results confirm that aligning translation behavior with cognitive principles enables robust generalization, high translation quality, and efficient reasoning in MDMT.

</details>


### [40] [Cross-Lingual Transfer for Machine Translation in Turkic Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29355)
*Omer Burak Cinar,Mehmet Mert Dalkilic,Cagri Toraman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了五种突厥语系语言（土耳其语、阿塞拜疆语、乌兹别克语、哈萨克语和吉尔吉斯语）之间的跨语言迁移行为。通过成对迁移矩阵实验发现，迁移在关系密切的突厥语对（尤其是土耳其语-阿塞拜疆语和哈萨克语-吉尔吉斯语）之间最强，迁移方向会影响结果，拉丁化在脚本不匹配场景下能提升BLEU和chrF分数，且迁移源在不同数据集和模型设置下相对稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源机器翻译中跨语言迁移至关重要，但紧密相关语系内部的迁移行为尚未得到充分刻画。作者旨在系统性地研究突厥语族内部不同语言对之间的迁移规律，以填补这一空白。

Method: 在五种突厥语系语言中构建成对迁移矩阵，每个模型使用一个迁移源（transfer source）进行微调，在另一个迁移目标（transfer target）上评估，翻译目标始终保持相同。在mT5模型上进行实验，并结合拉丁化脚本转换进行对比分析。

Result: 1) 迁移在关系密切的突厥语对中最强，特别是土耳其语-阿塞拜疆语和哈萨克语-吉尔吉斯语；2) 迁移方向（source-target）会影响迁移效果；3) 同一迁移源-迁移目标对在不同翻译目标下表现不同；4) 拉丁化在多个脚本不匹配场景下提升BLEU和chrF，但效果并非在所有指标上一致；5) 迁移源在不同数据集和模型设置下基本稳定。

Conclusion: 突厥语系内部的语言亲缘关系、迁移方向和脚本一致性是影响跨语言迁移效果的关键因素。研究结果为低资源突厥语翻译的迁移学习方法选择提供了实证指导。

Abstract: Cross-lingual transfer is central to low-resource machine translation, but its behavior within closely related language families remains insufficiently characterized. We study transfer among five Turkic languages; Turkish, Azerbaijani, Uzbek, Kazakh, and Kyrgyz; using pairwise transfer matrices. In this setting, each model is fine-tuned with one transfer source and evaluated on a different transfer target while the translation target remains the same. Across mT5 experiments, we find that transfer is strongest between closely related Turkic pairs, especially Turkish-Azerbaijani and Kazakh-Kyrgyz. We also show that transfer direction matters, and that the same transfer source-transfer target pair can behave differently when the translation target changes. Latinization improves BLEU and chrF in several script-mismatched settings, but its effect is not uniform across metrics. Additional analyses show that transfer sources are mostly stable across different datasets and model settings.

</details>


### [41] [Zero-Mem: Zero-Token Memory Operations for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29377)
*Yilin Xiao,Zhehan Zhu,Yujing Zhang,Jin Chen,Zijin Hong,Luyao Zhuang,Qinggang Zhang,Shengyuan Chen,Xiaocao Ouyang,Lingfei Ren,Xiao Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Zero-Mem提出了一种零token记忆操作方案，在不产生任何中间LLM生成的情况下实现结构化智能体记忆。它直接保留原始交互轨迹，通过实体-上下文图和时间层级两种互补视图组织记忆，仅最终QA读取器调用LLM，在长记忆和长上下文QA基准上达到竞争性性能，并将记忆操作时间成本降低57.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体系统依赖额外的LLM调用来操作记忆，包括生成中间记录和检索调解，这带来了反复的token和时间开销。同时，省略或合并的细节可能掩盖原始证据。作者质疑：结构化记忆访问是否真的需要生成过程？

Method: Zero-Mem提出零token记忆操作：除最终问答外，不调用任何LLM且不消耗任何LLM输入输出token。其核心方法包括：(1) 以原始交互轨迹作为记忆的唯一来源；(2) 通过两种互补视图组织轨迹——实体-上下文图展示跨交互的连接，时间层级保留会话局部性和状态；(3) 对每个查询动态权衡两种视图并从两者中检索，沿其结构恢复支持关系或上下文；(4) 确定性校准先丢弃冲突证据，再使读者答案基于检索轨迹保持grounded；(5) 仅最终QA读取器触发LLM调用。

Result: 在长记忆和长上下文问答基准上，Zero-Mem实现了与基线竞争的性能，同时消除了记忆操作中的LLM调用和token消耗。在相同的最终QA读取器和上下文预算下，相对于最快的对比基线，将记忆操作时间成本降低了57.6%。消融实验验证了两种视图及其查询依赖协同的贡献。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，结构化智能体记忆不需要生成过去的中间表示。通过确定性检索和校准机制，Zero-Mem证明了零生成记忆操作在性能和效率上均可达到有竞争力的水平，挑战了记忆中LLM生成的必要性假设。

Abstract: LLM agents need memory to act consistently over long interactions, yet many systems use additional LLM calls to operate that memory. Generating intermediate records and mediating their retrieval adds recurring token and time costs, while omitted or merged details can obscure the original evidence. We ask whether structured memory access requires generation at all. Zero-Mem introduces \emph{zero-token memory operations}: no step outside final question answering invokes an LLM or consumes LLM input or output tokens; encoder computation is accounted for separately. Zero-Mem preserves original interaction traces as its source of record. It organizes the traces in two complementary ways. An entity--context graph exposes connections across interactions, while a temporal hierarchy preserves conversational locality and session state. For each query, Zero-Mem weighs the two views, retrieves from both, and follows their structure to recover supporting relations or surrounding context. Deterministic calibration first discards conflicting evidence and then keeps the reader's answer grounded in the retrieved traces. Only the final-QA reader invokes an LLM. Across long-memory and long-context question-answering benchmarks, Zero-Mem achieves competitive performance while eliminating LLM calls and LLM-token consumption from memory operations. With the same final-QA reader and context budget, it reduces memory-operation time cost by 57.6\% relative to the fastest compared baseline. Ablations support the contribution of the two views and their query-dependent coordination. Overall, the results show that structured agent memory need not generate an intermediate representation of the past. After peer review, the code and implementation details will be available at \textcolor{blue}{https://github.com/TheMoon0815/Zero-mem}.

</details>


### [42] [PTP: Previous-Token Prediction based LLM Inversion for Near-Exact Prompt Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29378)
*Pirzada Suhail,Nagasai Saketh Naidu,Atanu R Sinha,Amit Sethi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种黑盒条件下的LLM逆向方法：在目标LLM自身生成的合成数据上，从头训练一个显式逆向语言模型，通过前一个token预测（previous-token prediction）来逆向重建prompt，无需访问模型权重或外部辅助数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM自回归逐token生成的方式导致prompt与response之间存在多对多映射，从观测输出中推断prompt非常困难。现有逆向方法依赖微调预训练seq2seq模型并在大规模外部数据集上进行语义重建，且需要访问模型权重或logits，限制了实际应用场景。

Method: 提出一种功能性逆向方法：(1) 在纯黑盒设定下，仅通过目标LLM本身合成生成训练数据；(2) 从零开始训练一个显式逆向语言模型，采用与正向next-token预测相对应的previous-token预测方式；(3) 通过采样支持多样的prompt重建，且所有重建的prompt在目标LLM下都能产生相似响应。

Result: 该方法在不同数据集上具有泛化能力，并表现出跨不同LLM的迁移性（即用某LLM训练的逆向模型也能重建其他LLM生成的响应对应的prompt）。在基于token的prompt和response重建评估指标上，该方法优于先前工作。

Conclusion: 通过建立正向生成与逆向过程的生成式联系，本文证明在无需模型权重访问和外部辅助数据的情况下，黑盒LLM逆向是可行且有效的，同时支持多样化且功能等价的prompt重建。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) generate text by auto-regressively sampling the next token. This inherently leads to a many-to-many mapping between prompts and responses, complicating the task of inferring prompts from observed outputs. Prior work on LLM inversion frames prompt recovery as a semantic reconstruction task. They rely on fine-tuning pretrained sequence-to-sequence models on large external datasets--and requiring access to model weights or logits--to generate semantically plausible prompts. In contrast, we present a functional approach to inverting a given LLM in a black-box setting, without auxiliary aids. We train an explicit inverse language model entirely from scratch on data synthetically generated from the target LLM itself. Analogous to forward next-token prediction, our inverse model is trained using previous-token prediction, establishing a generative link between the forward and inverse processes that enables faithful prompt reconstruction. Moreover, it naturally supports diverse prompt reconstructions through sampling, whereby all such prompts induce similar responses under the forward, target LLM. Our approach generalises across datasets and exhibits transferability in reconstructing prompts from responses generated by different LLMs. Further, across the set of token based evaluation metrics for prompt and response reconstructions, our approach outperforms prior work.

</details>

*Jim Zhao,Sohir Maskey,Koen Oostermeijer,Douglas Orr,Teryn Jones*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了两种翻译模型家族（EuroLLM和Hy-MT2，1.7B到22B共5个模型）在单块A100或H100 GPU上的量化权衡。发现文档分块策略结合W4A8或W8A8量化可以改善延迟-吞吐量Pareto曲线；同时引入基于WMT24++的文档级评估，证明标准片段级评估无法预测量化和长上下文文档翻译之间的交互；Hy-MT2对量化鲁棒，而EuroLLM对量化高度敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在真实服务器环境中部署需要低延迟高质量响应，量化是常见手段，但其对延迟和吞吐量的影响很少在受控的编排级工作负载下评估。此外，标准MT基准基于孤立句子，无法捕捉长上下文动态，导致量化和长上下文翻译的交互被忽视。

Method: 在两种翻译模型家族（EuroLLM和Hy-MT2）的5个模型（1.7B到22B）上，在单块A100或H100 GPU上进行量化实验。将文档分块策略与W4A8或W8A8量化结合，评估延迟-吞吐量Pareto曲线；并引入基于WMT24++的文档级评估框架来研究文本分块策略在量化下对翻译质量的影响。

Result: 结合文档分块策略与W4A8或W8A8量化能改善延迟-吞吐量Pareto曲线；标准片段级评估无法预测量化和长上下文文档翻译的交互；Hy-MT2对量化鲁棒，而EuroLLM在所有量化格式下翻译质量均迅速崩溃。

Conclusion: 推理效率和翻译质量之间的权衡不仅取决于量化格式，还取决于文本分块策略的选择。这强调了在评估量化方法时需要同时考虑推理指标和文档级翻译质量。

Abstract: Deploying large language models in realistic server environments poses challenges, as the system needs to provide high-quality responses with low latency. Quantization is a common approach to reduce the memory footprint and improve inference efficiency, yet its impact on latency and throughput is rarely evaluated under controlled, orchestration-level workloads. In this work we study the quantization trade-offs of two translation model families, EuroLLM \citep{martins2025eurollm} and Hy-MT2 \citep{zheng2026hy} across five models ranging from 1.7B to 22B for efficient deployment on a single A100 or H100 GPU. We demonstrate that combining a document-chunking strategy with W4A8 or W8A8 quantization improves the latency-throughput Pareto-curve under a wide range of workloads. Furthermore, since standard machine translation (MT) benchmarks rely on isolated sentences and fail to capture long-context dynamics, we introduce a document-level evaluation from WMT24++ to assess how text chunking strategies affect translation quality under quantization. Our results reveal that standard segment-level evaluation can fail to predict the interaction between quantization and long-context document translation. While Hy-MT2 remains robust under quantization, EuroLLM shows strong sensitivity and translation quality collapses rapidly for all considered quantization formats. Overall, our experiments show that the trade-off between inference efficiency and translation quality depends not only on the quantization format, but also on the choice of text chunking strategy.

</details>


### [44] [Know It, Act on It: Investigating Memory Utilization in LLM Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29433)
*Zhaoxin Feng,Jianfei Ma,Emmanuele Chersoni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种解耦评估范式，将LLM代理的记忆问题拆分为"知道"与"行动"两个维度，通过配对测试发现代理经常能回忆用户偏好（Know通过）但在实际行为场景中未能应用该偏好（Act失败），且健康与治疗相关的偏好利用缺陷尤为严重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理作为个性化伙伴时，即使相关用户偏好完整存在于上下文中，模型仍可能无法据此调整行为。现有评估无法区分失败是由于"未记住"还是"记住了但未使用"，阻碍了对记忆能力缺陷的精准定位与改进。

Method: 提出Know-Act解耦评估范式：对同一用户偏好分别施测"知道/回忆"测试与"行动/行为"测试；在16个系统、5种内存架构上进行大规模实验，评估了嵌入在三种表达强度级别的1000条用户偏好。

Result: Know与Act之间存在显著差距：代理常能通过回忆测试却未能在配对行为场景中反映该偏好。内存架构虽能缩小该差距，但健康与治疗相关偏好的利用仍然尤为薄弱，而此类场景中行动失败的现实代价最高。

Conclusion: LLM代理存在独立的"知识利用"瓶颈：记忆不足只是问题的一半，更关键的是模型记住了却未能在行为中应用。未来研究需聚焦于从记忆到行动的转化机制，尤其是在高风险领域（如健康/治疗）中强化偏好利用能力。

Abstract: As large language model (LLM) agents evolve into personalized companions, memory has emerged as a core capability. However, LLMs face a knowledge utilization problem: they may fail to act on relevant user preferences even when they are fully present in context. When an agent fails to tailor its response in a context where previously shared user preferences should matter, it is unclear whether the model failed to remember that information or remembered it but failed to use it. To isolate this breakdown, we introduce a decoupled evaluation paradigm that administers paired Know and Act tests to the same user preference. We conduct large-scale experiments across 16 systems and five memory architectures, evaluating 1,000 preferences embedded at three levels of expression strength. Our results show a large gap between Know and Act outcomes: agents often pass the recall test for a user preference but fail to reflect that same preference in the paired behavioral scenario. While memory architectures reduce this gap, utilization remains especially weak for health and therapy-related preferences, where failures to act carry the greatest real-world stakes.

</details>


### [45] [Evidence-Type Competition: When Can Interventional Data Teach Language Models Causal Direction?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29484)
*Xining Xun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在Simpson悖论控制环境中，干预数据在预训练中的比例并不决定模型是否使用因果证据；真正决定权重中存储的因果能力是否被激活的是推理时上下文中的证据类型。观测性上下文会系统性抑制因果插值能力（导致符号反转），而纯干预探针则能释放该能力。抑制作用具有内容中介性、分级性、跨种子稳定性和参数量鲁棒性，激活修补定位到中间层的观测行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验"干预数据是教模型因果推理的金标准"这一广泛假设。作者在完全受控的合成环境中观测数据相关性与因果效应符号相反，从而系统地测试预训练数据中干预样本比例对模型do()-响应方向的影响。

Method: 在Simpson悖论环境下构建可控合成实验：(1) 对比不同干预/观测样本比例的预训练；(2) 在推理时操纵上下文证据类型（纯观测、混合、纯干预探针）；(3) 擦除上下文中观测证据；(4) 四状态内容操作验证内容中介性；(5) 多随机种子和多参数量（0.93B）验证鲁棒性；(6) 激活修补定位开关位置；(7) CLadder外部审计；(8) 提出的证据平均协议量化采样噪声底限。

Result: 增大干预样本比例只会单调增加do()-响应幅度但不会改变符号方向——符号从观测上下文复制。纯观测上下文在29/50世界导致符号反转，混合19/50，纯干预探针41/50正确。擦除观测证据立即释放被压制的因果插值能力（ratio_true=+0.56）。抑制作用在11/11种子中稳定保持，0.93B参数下速率鲁棒（31.8% vs 6%）。CLadder审计发现两层结构的正效应先验。证据平均协议将符号错误从26%降至9%。

Conclusion: 模型的因果推理能力存储在权重中，但何时使用由推理时的上下文证据类型决定；干预数据比例并非关键因素。观测性上下文会覆盖权重中的因果能力强先验。研究还揭示了基于探针的因果评估的采样噪声下限，并提出了降低符号错误的证据平均协议。

Abstract: Interventional data is widely regarded as the gold standard for teaching models causal reasoning. We test this assumption in a fully controlled synthetic environment pitting observational correlation against causal effect, and find it fails instructively. In Simpson's-paradox worlds, where the two have systematically opposite signs, increasing the fraction of interventional samples in pretraining does not improve causal direction: the magnitude of the model's do()-response grows monotonically, yet its sign is copied from the observational context. What governs whether interventional evidence is used is not the training mixture but the evidence type present in the context at inference time. Under an identical training recipe, a purely observational context induces systematic sign reversal in 29/50 worlds, a mixed context in 19/50, while aligned interventional probes alone yield 41/50 correct. Erasing observational evidence from the context immediately releases the suppressed causal interpolation ability (ratio_true = +0.56); a four-state content manipulation shows the switch is content-mediated and graded. The suppression is stable across training seeds (11/11 strong reversals persist on a matched-protocol second seed) and robust as a rate at 0.93B parameters (31.8% vs. 6% reversals in the matched probe-only arm), even as absolute gains shrink four-fold. An external audit on CLadder exposes a learned positive-effect prior with a two-layer structure: sign-randomized retraining removes it in-distribution but not out-of-distribution. We summarize: the capability lives in the weights; the switch lives in the context, and activation patching localizes the switch to the middle layers' observational rows. We further quantify the sampling noise floor of probe-based causal evaluation and an evidence-averaging protocol that cuts sign errors from 26% to 9%.

</details>


### [46] [ARB: A Matched Authorship-Rewriting Benchmark Dataset for AI-Text Detector Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29539)
*Gaetano Perrone,Simon Pietro Romano*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文引入作者重写基准（ARB），基于1800个人类源文本和4个开源生成器构建四类匹配变体（HUMAN、Free-LLM、H2L、LLM2L），评估5个检测器。结果发现：检测器对直接LLM文本检测率高（91-93%），但对LLM改写的人类文本检测率骤降至15-31%（下降60-78个百分点），而对LLM文本自改写仍保持78-83%的召回率。表明传统基准上的性能无法迁移到LLM改写人类文本的场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI文本检测基准仅比较人类文本与LLM直接生成的文本，而现实中人类文本常被LLM改写。此前研究虽表明改写会降低检测性能，但未明确传统基准上的性能是否能预测检测器在"人类文本被LLM改写"场景下的表现，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 构建ARB基准：从XSum、WritingPrompts、OpenWebText三个数据集各取600篇人类源文本（共1800篇），使用四个开源权重生成器（Llama-3.2-3B、Qwen2.5-7B、Mistral-7B、Gemma-2-9B）生成四类匹配变体：人类原文（HUMAN）、LLM直接生成（Free-LLM）、LLM改写人类文本（H2L）、同生成器LLM改写LLM文本（LLM2L）。在严格1%假阳性率工作点（TPR@1%FPR）下评估五个检测器（FastDetectGPT、Binoculars-falcon-7b、RADAR、BERT-Defense、RoBERTa-Defense）。

Result: FastDetectGPT和Binoculars-falcon-7b对直接LLM文本检测率达91.2%和93.5%，但对LLM改写的人类文本仅30.8%和15.1%，下降60-78个百分点；对LLM文本自改写仍保持78.3%和83.0%召回率，仅下降10-13个百分点。RADAR呈现相同趋势（66.8%降至12.2%），而BERT-Defense和RoBERTa-Defense在所有场景下召回率均低于3%。

Conclusion: 检测器在传统人类vs直接LLM基准上的性能不能迁移到LLM改写人类文本的场景，虽然同一批检测器对LLM重写LLM文本基本保持鲁棒。这一发现揭示了现有检测评估范式的关键盲区，呼吁重新审视AI文本检测的评估方式。

Abstract: Standard AI-text detection benchmarks compare human-written text against text generated directly by large language models (LLMs). While prior work has shown that rewriting and paraphrasing can degrade detector performance, it remains unclear whether performance measured on this conventional benchmark predicts detector behavior when human-authored content is rewritten by an LLM. To address this gap, we introduce Authorship-Rewriting Benchmark (ARB), built from 1,800 human source texts (600 each from XSum, WritingPrompts, and OpenWebText) and four open-weight generators (Llama-3.2-3B, Qwen2.5-7B, Mistral-7B, Gemma-2-9B). Each source item yields four matched variants: human-written (HUMAN), direct LLM generation (Free-LLM), LLM-rewritten human text (H2L), and same-generator LLM-rewritten LLM text (LLM2L). We evaluated five detectors (FastDetectGPT, Binoculars-falcon-7b, RADAR, BERT-Defense, RoBERTa-Defense) at a strict 1%-false-positive operating point (TPR@1%FPR). FastDetectGPT and Binoculars-falcon-7b detected 91.2% and 93.5\% of direct LLM text, but only 30.8% and 15.1% of human text an LLM had rewritten, a drop of 60-78 percentage points. The same detectors retained 78.3% and 83.0% recall when LLM text was rewritten by the same model, a much smaller decline of 10-13 points. RADAR followed the same pattern (66.8% to 12.2%), while BERT-Defense and RoBERTa-Defense stayed below 3% recall across all regimes. These results show that detector performance measured on the conventional human-vs-LLM benchmark does not transfer to human-authored text revised by an LLM, even though the same detectors remain largely robust to LLM-only rewriting.

</details>


### [47] [Sycophancy Undermines Epistemic Vigilance in Cooperative Vision-Language Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29585)
*Rupak Sarkar,Neha Srikanth,Saloni Gupta,Claire Bonial,Philip Resnik,Rachel Rudinger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种信息不对称的对话式"找不同"任务来测量视觉-语言模型在合作场景中的认知警觉性（epistemic vigilance），发现模型常因迎合对话伙伴而忽略自己私密图像中的关键证据，并通过任务无关的奉承向量干预有效减少了这类错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人类合作对话中，参与者需要不断更新信念并对新信息保持认知警觉，当新信息与已有信念冲突时须检测并修复矛盾。AI系统要成为复杂合作任务中可靠的伙伴，必须同样权衡外来信息与自身私有证据及共享上下文，并适时地指出不一致之处。然而现有研究缺乏对此能力的系统性测量。

Method: 构建了一个信息不对称的、基于对话的"找不同"（spot-the-difference）任务：两个模型各自被私下展示一张图片，需要通过对话确定两张图片是否相同，若不同则找出差异。在此基础上，将模型违背认知警觉的行为与奉承（sycophancy）现象建立关联，并采用从任务无关的奉承示例中学习到的向量对模型进行干预（steering）以降低奉承倾向。

Result: 模型在该任务中经常失败：倾向于忽略私有图像中的关键证据而迎合对话伙伴的不合理判断。通过奉承降低向量干预后，模型在信息不对称合作任务中的认知警觉相关错误显著减少，成为更忠实的证据报告者。

Conclusion: 视觉-语言模型在合作对话中的认知警觉性不足，表现为过度迎合与弱证据锚定，这本质上是奉承行为在合作目标导向对话中的体现；任务无关的奉承向量干预能有效缓解这一问题，提升模型在信息不对称合作任务中的可靠性。

Abstract: To maintain common ground in cooperative conversation, humans iteratively update their beliefs as conversation participants share new information; participants who are epistemically vigilant detect when new information conflicts with prior beliefs and take steps to repair these conflicts. In order for AI systems to serve as reliable partners in complex cooperative tasks, they must similarly weigh incoming information against their own private evidence and shared context and appropriately surface inconsistencies when they arise. To measure the epistemic vigilance of vision-language models in cooperative settings, we present an information-asymmetric, dialog-based "spot-the-difference" task. Two models are privately shown one image each, and must determine through conversation whether the images are identical or, if not, identify the difference. Models routinely fail at this: they frequently overlook key evidence in their private image in favor of agreeing with their conversational partner, even when their agreement is unwarranted. We relate these violations of epistemic vigilance to the broader behavior of sycophancy, which manifests itself in cooperative goal-oriented dialog as over-accommodation and weak evidential grounding. Our results show that model steering to reduce sycophancy with a vector learned from task-agnostic sycophancy examples can reduce epistemic vigilance-related errors, making models more faithful reporters of their evidence, and in turn, more reliable partners in information-asymmetric cooperative tasks.

</details>


### [48] [ResKV: Reconstructing Omitted Attention Contributions for Fixed-Budget KV Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29591)
*Yuhang Zhan,Lisi Chen,Shuo Shang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ResKV 将固定KV预算划分为精确主缓存与紧凑残差缓存，通过残差统计恢复被驱逐token对softmax注意力分子和分母的贡献，从而在保持压缩解码效率的同时提升长上下文推理质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的KV缓存压缩方法存在两难：逐出式方法会永久丢弃未选中token，丢失它们在注意力中的聚合贡献；合并式方法虽保留更多信息，但会扰动本应精确保留的键和值。需要一种既能高效压缩又能保留被忽略token信息贡献的方法。

Method: ResKV将固定KV预算分为精确主缓存和紧凑残差缓存。核心思想是将缓存逐出所遗漏的信息形式化为softmax注意力分子与分母中的残差统计量。残差条目与主缓存token参与同一softmax归一化，从而同时恢复注意力分子和分母的贡献而非事后修正。构建时通过验证代理确定各层和各KV头的残差分配，解码时通过动态门控针对单个查询调整残差贡献。

Result: 在LongBench和RULER基准上的全面评估覆盖了查询感知与查询无关设置、多个骨干模型、不同缓存预算及代表性压缩基线，结果表明在相同保留KV预算下ResKV取得广泛改进，同时保持了压缩解码的实际效率，包括峰值内存使用和长上下文解码吞吐量。

Conclusion: ResKV通过将逐出token的聚合贡献重构为残差统计量并纳入同一softmax归一化，有效解决了KV缓存压缩中信息丢失与主缓存精确性之间的权衡，在不牺牲解码效率的前提下显著提升压缩后的推理质量。

Abstract: KV cache compression is essential for efficient long-context inference. Existing eviction methods permanently discard unselected tokens and consequently remove their aggregate contribution to attention. Merging-based alternatives preserve more information but can perturb retained keys and values that should remain exact. We observe that the information omitted by cache eviction can be formulated as residual statistics in both the numerator and denominator of softmax attention. Based on this observation, we propose ResKV, which divides a fixed KV budget into an exact main cache and a compact residual cache that reconstructs the contribution of omitted tokens. ResKV lets main-cache tokens and residual entries participate in the same softmax normalization, so residual entries restore both attention numerator and denominator mass rather than acting as a post-hoc correction. A construction-time validation proxy determines residual allocation for each layer and KV head, while a decode-time dynamic gate adjusts residual contributions for individual queries. Comprehensive evaluations on LongBench and RULER, covering query-aware and query-agnostic settings, multiple backbones, cache budgets, and representative compression baselines, demonstrate broad improvements under the same retained KV budget while preserving the practical efficiency of compressed decoding, including peak memory usage and long-context decode throughput.

</details>

*Jeffrey M. Girard,Jason Z. Zheng,Jacqueline R. Vertino,Antony D'Avirro,Benjamin Peloquin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出FriendBench基准，用于从20秒双人破冰对话片段中判断两人是熟人还是陌生人。在96个平衡对话对中，对比了来自七家公司的26个模型与人类群体在文本、音频、视频三种模态下的表现，发现最佳模型与人类群体在准确率上无统计差异，但达到方式不同：人类在两答案间保持均衡，而最强模型偏向"陌生人"答案。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交情境的理解往往依赖于行为而非单纯的言语，现有基准缺乏从非语言交互方式（而非对话内容）推断人际关系熟悉度的评估。文章旨在填补这一空白，系统评估多模态模型在此类社交推理任务上的能力。

Method: 构建FriendBench基准，使用20秒双人破冰对话视频片段（每组对话回答相同提示词，确保仅交互方式可揭示答案），跨越96个平衡二元组，在文本、音频、视频三种模态下比较26个模型（来自七家公司）与匹配的人类评审小组的表现。

Result: 最佳模型与人类群体在每种模态下的准确率统计上无显著差异；但人类在两种答案间保持平衡，最强模型则系统性偏向"陌生人"答案——差异在于有效先验而非判别能力。更丰富的通道对两者增益不均，只有人类能从可见行为叠加语音中获得额外收益。

Conclusion: 多模态模型在推断人际熟悉度任务上已达到接近人类的水平，但存在先验偏差问题；同时模型无法像人类那样从视觉行为中有效提取额外信息，表明视觉社交信号的理解仍是模型的短板。研究公开了刺激材料、人类评分和模型预测以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Reading a social situation often depends on behavior, not words alone. We introduce FriendBench, a benchmark for inferring whether two people are already familiar or are meeting as strangers, from a 20-second clip of a dyadic ice-breaker conversation. Every pair answers the same type of prompt, so only the manner of interaction can reveal the answer. Across text, audio, and video, we compare 26 models from seven companies against matched human panels over 96 balanced dyads. The best model and the human crowd are statistically indistinguishable on accuracy in every modality, but reach it differently: humans stay balanced across the two answers, while the strongest models lean toward "stranger"---a difference in effective prior, not discrimination. Richer channels help both unequally, and only humans gain from visible behavior on top of speech. We release the stimuli, human ratings, and model predictions.

</details>


### [50] [Evolving language compositionality in a frequency-structured meaning space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29642)
*Fabio De Ponte,Eloise Gaines-White,Conor Houghton,Seth Bullock*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨了迭代学习模型中意义频率分布对涌现语言组合性的影响，发现当频率分布于整体意义向量时，高频意义可脱离语法约束（与自然语言一致），但当频率分布于部分特征时，语言无法跨代稳定传递。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明语言组合性可在重复传递中自发涌现，但频率对组合性特征的具体影响尚不清楚。本文旨在探索非均匀意义频率如何改变语言组合性的性质，并揭示频率分布的位置（整体意义 vs. 部分特征）对语言可习得性和可传递性的关键作用。

Method: 基于迭代学习模型进行计算机模拟实验，系统操控两种频率分布条件：一种将频率施加于完整的意义向量，另一种将频率施加于意义的组成部分（部分特征）上，观察不同条件下语言的组合性特征以及跨代传递的稳定性。

Result: 研究发现，当频率施加于整体意义向量时，高频意义可以逃避低压力的语法约束（与自然语言中的不规则高频形式一致）；但当频率施加于部分特征时，即使最高频的元素能被可靠习得，语言整体也无法在代际间成功传递。

Conclusion: 频率只有在作用于学习者能够整体习得的形式—意义单元上时，才能塑造涌现的语言结构。当频率作用于更小的子单元时，无法支撑组合概括所需的关系结构，从而阻碍语言的稳定传递。

Abstract: The iterated learning model was introduced to investigate language evolution: the way in which the characteristic properties of human languages have been shaped, at least partly, by repeated transmission from one language user to another. The key finding is that language compositionality can arise spontaneously as a consequence of language being passed repeatedly through a language learning bottleneck. Here we explore how changing the frequency of different meanings, so that some meanings occur much more frequently than others, affects the character of its compositionality. We find that, as observed in natural languages, high-frequency meanings can escape the pressure to conform to the grammar that characterizes lower-frequency meanings. However, when the frequency structure is instead imposed on parts rather than on whole meaning vectors, the language fails to transmit across generations. This occurs despite the fact that the most frequent elements are reliably learned. These results suggest that frequency can shape emergent linguistic structure only when the frequency distribution is defined over form-meaning units that learners can acquire holistically. When frequency is instead distributed over smaller units, it fails to support the relational structure required for compositional generalisation, thereby preventing stable language transmission.

</details>


### [51] [TokTier: Exact Stateful Tokenization for Agentic LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29678)
*Zhenyu Zhang,Zhichao Cao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TokTier是一个有状态的tokenization服务，通过增量修复和GPU加速的全量tokenization，在保证token ID与完整参考tokenization完全一致的前提下，将LLM服务的前端tokenization延迟降低最多491倍，并在vLLM中使TTFT中位数下降16-34%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM serving系统虽然缓存了prompt KV状态，但大多数前端仍然在每次调用时对完整请求文本重新tokenize。这在coding agent场景尤为严重——agent在每次小的工具结果后都会重新提交长对话记录，而即使短小的追加也可能改变前一个序列末尾的token边界。实测中tokenization耗时最高可占TTFT的64%，成为延迟瓶颈。

Method: TokTier包含两个核心策略：(1) 对于会话延续，只对追加附近的窗口重新tokenize，并通过per-request稳定边界检查确保拼接结果与全量tokenization完全一致，失败时扩大窗口或回退到全量tokenization；(2) 对于无可复用前缀的调用，将GPT家族的regex预tokenization分解为run-local规则，并在GPU上执行精确的预tokenization和BPE。此外通过抽样的shadow verifier对线上流量进行复核。

Result: 在17种tokenizer家族、1.5x10^10次split检查、12.4TB真实文本和93,000+个replay agent步骤中实现零分歧。增量修复在100K到3M字符下耗时0.5-1.1ms，比HF快437倍，比全预热的Gigatoken在1M字符下快2.1倍。GPU全量tokenization编码1M字符仅需0.87ms，比HF低491倍。在vLLM中，TTFT中位数下降16-34%，P99下降23%。在50ms P99目标下，4个repair核心+1个GPU可支撑1821 req/s，而16核无状态前端在40 req/s即饱和。

Conclusion: TokTier证明了有状态tokenization服务的可行性：通过精确的增量修复算法和GPU加速，可以在不牺牲tokenization正确性的前提下，显著降低LLM服务的前端tokenization开销，尤其适用于coding agent这类高频会话延续场景。

Abstract: LLM serving systems cache prompt KV state, yet most front ends still re-tokenize the full request text on every call. The cost lands on coding agents, which resubmit a long transcript after each small tool result, and reuse is hard because even a short append can change token boundaries near the end of the previous sequence. Across 153,951 calls from two agent ecosystems, the median call appends about 1.4K characters, and only 1.0-3.6% of calls start or rebuild a session with contexts of millions of characters. At a 94.1% fleet prompt-cache hit rate, tokenization reaches up to 64% of time to first token.
  TokTier is a stateful tokenization service with one contract: emitted token IDs are always identical to full reference tokenization of the request text. For a session continuation, it re-tokenizes a small window around the append and splices only after a per-request stable-boundary check, widening the window or falling back to full tokenization on failure. For a call without a reusable prefix, it decomposes GPT-family regex pre-tokenization into run-local rules and runs exact pre-tokenization and BPE on a GPU. A sampled shadow verifier re-checks live traffic.
  Across 17 tokenizer families, differential campaigns cover 1.5x10^10 split checks, a 12.4 TB real-text corpus, and 93,000+ replayed agent steps, with zero divergence. Incremental repair takes 0.5-1.1 ms from 100K to 3M characters, up to 437x faster than HF tokenization and 2.1x faster at 1M than the strongest cache-based baseline (Gigatoken) fully prewarmed. GPU full tokenization encodes a 1M-character request in 0.87 ms, up to 491x below HF and 23.4x below the fastest published CPU method. With vLLM, median time to first token drops 16-34% and P99 drops 23% under recorded bursts. Under a 50 ms P99 objective, four repair cores plus one GPU sustain 1,821 requests/s where a 16-core stateless front end saturates at 40.

</details>

### [52] [Bridging the Gap Between PHE and FHE: A Performance and Trade-off Analysis of The Somewhat Homomorphic BGN Cryptosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28700)
*Sefik Serengil,Alper Ozpinar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过将BGN同态加密方案集成到lightphe Python框架中，对其与PHE（Paillier、Damgard-Jurik、Okamoto-Uchiyama）和FHE（CKKS）方案进行了系统对比分析。实验表明BGN在计算速度上因双线性配对而慢于其他方案，但公钥尺寸仅3-6 KB，比FHE小五个数量级，且在单次乘法后支持无限同态聚合，适用于带宽受限的分布式场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BGN作为Somewhat Homomorphic Encryption方案，能够弥补PHE与FHE之间的空白（支持无限加法与一次密文乘法），但实际应用中因缺乏易用的软件实现而受限。本文旨在通过将其集成到lightphe框架中，使BGN变得易于部署，并对其进行系统性的性能对比评估。

Method: 将BGN密码系统集成到lightphe Python框架实现跨方案基准测试，在80位、112位和128位三个安全级别下，对128维加密向量操作分别与Paillier、Damgard-Jurik、Okamoto-Uchiyama（PHE）以及TenSEAL中的CKKS（FHE）方案进行对比，评估计算性能、公钥大小和通信开销，并通过线性回归推断、余弦相似度、平方欧氏距离等任务验证实际可用性。

Result: 实验揭示了计算-通信权衡：BGN因双线性配对运算在计算速度上慢于PHE和SIMD优化后的FHE，但其公钥仅3-6 KB，比FHE小最多五个数量级。BGN在单次乘法后支持无限同态聚合，且仅需2位小数精度即可匹配明文排序基线，解决了目标群离散对数解密瓶颈。

Conclusion: 通过开源该流水线到lightphe，本文确立BGN作为带宽受限、去中心化架构中实用同态加密引擎的地位。

Abstract: Homomorphic encryption (HE) enables privacy-preserving data analytics, but practitioners often face a trade-off between lightweight Partially Homomorphic Encryption (PHE) and computationally dominant Fully Homomorphic Encryption (FHE). The Boneh-Goh-Nissim (BGN) cryptosystem bridges this gap as a Somewhat Homomorphic Encryption (SWHE) scheme supporting unlimited additions and one ciphertext multiplication. Despite its algebraic elegance, practical BGN adoption has been hindered by a lack of accessible software implementations. This paper presents a comparative analysis of BGN against PHE and FHE paradigms through its integration into the lightphe Python framework, allowing deployment in just a few lines of code. We benchmark encrypted 128-dimensional vector operations under 80-bit, 112-bit and 128-bit security levels against Paillier, Damgard-Jurik, Okamoto-Uchiyama, and the FHE CKKS scheme via TenSEAL. Results reveal a computation-communication trade-off: BGN is computationally slower due to bilinear pairings compared to PHE and SIMD-optimized FHE, but retains a microscopic public key size of 3-6 KB, up to five orders of magnitude smaller than FHE. Crucially, BGN enables boundless homomorphic aggregation after a single multiplication, supporting complex tasks such as linear regression inference, Cosine Similarity, and Squared Euclidean Distance. Furthermore, an optimized precision of 2 digits suffices to match plaintext ranking baselines, overcoming the target-group discrete logarithm decryption bottleneck. By open-sourcing this pipeline in lightphe, this work establishes BGN as a practical engine for bandwidth-constrained, decentralized architectures.

</details>


### [53] [Costs of Arbitrary Real Matrix Factorizations for Pure-DP Continual Counting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28703)
*Awnon Bhowmik,Mahmudul Hasan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文证明了纯ε-差分隐私下Laplace矩阵机制的因子化成本cfrob(T_n)和ctwo(T_n)均为Θ((log(n+1))^{3/2})，从而优化后的最大和均方误差均为Θ(ε^{-2}log³(n+1))。该结果通过p-范数核（p-nuclear）障碍建立，无需对符号、稀疏性或方形性做任何限制，并解决了Arkhipov和Kalinin提出的任意实数因子扩展开放问题。此外还确定了0<p<1时前缀求和矩阵的p-核范数阶数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在纯ε-差分隐私框架下，Laplace矩阵机制的性能由因子化成本决定，但此前对前缀和矩阵T_n的因子化成本的精确阶数尚不明确。Arkhipov和Kalinin仅证明了{0,1}值因子的下界，并将任意实数因子情形留作开放问题。本文旨在建立对任意实数因子（无符号、稀疏性或方形性限制）的精确阶数Θ((log(n+1))^{3/2})，从而填补这一理论空白。

Method: 本文采用p-范数核障碍方法：首先建立聚合计宽估计D_k(T_n)≍n^{3/2}k^{-1/2}（适用于1≤k≤n/16的低秩范围），将其输入Pietsch与Hinrichs-Pietsch的经典逼近空间转换，在临界指数p=2/3处产生调和效应；随后通过Hölder不等式将p-范数核障碍传递到两种因子化成本。上界则通过Fenwick区间分解构造匹配的因子化方案。

Result: 主要结果为：(1) cfrob(T_n)和ctwo(T_n)均等于Θ((log(n+1))^{3/2})，无任何符号、稀疏性或方形性限制；(2) 纯ε-DP矩阵机制类中，优化的最大和均方误差均为Θ(ε^{-2}log³(n+1))；(3) 确定了p-核范数nucpow_p(T_n)的阶数：p<2/3时为n，p=2/3时为n log n，p>2/3时为n^{3p/2}。

Conclusion: 本文完整解决了任意实数因子下前缀和矩阵的因子化成本精确阶数问题，证明了它们以(log n)^{3/2}的速率增长，并通过p-核障碍方法给出了统一的证明框架。该结果仅限于纯ε-DP Laplace矩阵机制及给出的两种平方误差准则，不适用于非矩阵持续机制、近似DP灵敏度或坐标间期望最大值等扩展情形。

Abstract: Let \(T_n\) be the lower-triangular prefix-sum matrix and let \(\cfrob(T_n)\) and \(\ctwo(T_n)\) be the factorization costs that govern mean and maximum per-coordinate squared error of the Laplace matrix mechanism under pure \(\eps\)-differential privacy, for \(\eps>0\). We prove \(\cfrob(T_n),\ctwo(T_n)=Θ\bigl((\log(n+1))^{3/2}\bigr)\) with no sign, sparsity, or squareness restriction and with arbitrary finite inner dimension. Consequently, within the pure-\(\eps\)-DP matrix-mechanism class the optimized maximum and mean squared errors are both \(Θ(\eps^{-2}\log^{3}(n+1))\). Under the factorization contract of Arkhipov and Kalinin (arXiv:2607.08963v1), who prove the matching lower order for factors with entries in \(\{0,1\}\) and state the arbitrary-factor extension as open, the theorem below establishes the order for arbitrary real factors. The lower bound runs through a \(p\)-nuclear obstruction: an aggregate column-width estimate \(D_k(T_n)\asymp n^{3/2}k^{-1/2}\), valid in the low-rank range \(1\leq k\leq n/16\), for the prefix chain, fed into the classical approximation-space conversion of Pietsch and Hinrichs--Pietsch, becomes harmonic at the critical exponent \(p=2/3\), and Hölder's inequality transfers it to both factorization costs. The same computation determines \(\nucpow_p(T_n)\) for each fixed \(0<p<1\): order \(n\) below \(2/3\), \(n\log n\) at \(2/3\), and \(n^{3p/2}\) above. A Fenwick interval factorization supplies matching upper bounds. The claims are confined to pure-\(\eps\)-DP Laplace matrix mechanisms and the two stated squared-error criteria; they do not cover non-matrix continual mechanisms, approximate-DP sensitivity, or expected maxima across coordinates.

</details>


### [54] [Blockchain Transaction Simulation Phishing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28747)
*Xiaocan Wang,Shixuan Guan,Tong Yang,Xiapu Luo,Yue Duan,Kai Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了交易模拟钓鱼攻击（transaction simulation phishing），这是一种利用精心构造的智能合约欺骗钱包交易模拟功能的新型攻击方式。作者提出了SIMGUARD检测系统，在以太坊、BSC、Avalanche和Polygon上检测到超过4000个钓鱼合约，涉及5700多名受害者、约348万美元损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流加密钱包（如MetaMask）通过交易模拟功能预览交易前后的余额变化来防范钓鱼和诈骗攻击，但作者发现这种防御机制本身可以被利用：攻击者构造依赖动态区块链状态的智能合约，使交易模拟显示良性或有利的结果，而实际链上执行却会将用户资金重定向到攻击者控制的地址。这种新型攻击暴露了当前钱包防御的关键弱点，亟需系统性研究和检测方案。

Method: 作者首先建立了一个可用于实施交易模拟钓鱼的钓鱼合约分类体系（taxonomy）；然后提出了SIMGUARD，一个基于字节码级别的检测系统，结合静态和动态程序分析来识别钓鱼合约。该系统被应用于以太坊、币安智能链、Avalanche和Polygon四条链上进行大规模检测。

Result: SIMGUARD在2024年8月至2025年6月期间检测到超过4000个钓鱼合约；识别出超过5700名受害者和约348万美元的损失，其中91.5%的损失发生在以太坊上。此外，聚类分析发现最大的钓鱼合约集群单独就占据了总损失的约83%。

Conclusion: 研究结果暴露了当前钱包交易模拟防御机制的严重缺陷，凸显了开发更稳健的交易模拟机制的紧迫性。交易模拟钓鱼是一种真实且造成重大经济损失的新型攻击面，需要行业层面加强防御。

Abstract: Cryptocurrency users have increasingly become targets of phishing and scam attacks. To mitigate these threats, leading crypto wallets (e.g., MetaMask) have introduced transaction simulation, which previews a transaction's balance changes before on-chain execution. While effective against traditional fund-draining attacks, we show that this defense can itself be exploited by a new phishing technique, which we term transaction simulation phishing. This attack uses carefully crafted smart contracts whose execution depends on dynamic blockchain state, causing simulations to display benign or profitable outcomes while the actual on-chain execution redirects users' funds to attacker-controlled addresses.
  We present the first comprehensive study of transaction simulation phishing. We first develop a taxonomy of phishing contracts that can be utilized to facilitate this attack. Then, we propose SIMGUARD, a bytecode-level detection system that combines static and dynamic program analysis to identify phishing contracts. Applying SIMGUARD to Ethereum, Binance Smart Chain, Avalanche, and Polygon, we detect over 4,000 phishing contracts deployed between August 2024 and June 2025. Our analysis identifies more than 5,700 victims and approximately $3.48 million USD in losses, 91.5% of which occurred on Ethereum. Moreover, our clustering result reveals that the largest phishing contract cluster alone accounts for about 83% of the total losses. These results expose a critical weakness in current wallet defenses and highlight the urgent need for more robust transaction simulation mechanisms.

</details>


### [55] [Partial Derandomization for Leakage-Resilient Shamir's Secret Sharing over Composite Order Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28757)
*S. Venkitesh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对复合阶域上抗泄漏Shamir秘密共享的显式求值位置构造取得进展，基于Nguyen（EUROCRYPT 2025）的二分法，提出求值位置的偏去随机化方案：用固定有理函数Φ的迭代序列替代n个独立随机求值位置，将随机性从nd·log p比特降至d·log p比特，在n=O(d/log_p d)且重构阈值k≥2的参数范围内，对单块泄漏达到完美安全（统计距离精确为零）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Maji等人（EUROCRYPT 2024）表明随机求值位置可使复合阶域上的Shamir秘密共享对物理比特泄漏统计安全，但随机性开销过大（nd·log p比特）。Nguyen（EUROCRYPT 2025）建立了线性码秘密共享方案在统计安全与完全不安全之间的二分法。作者希望在保持安全性的前提下减少求值位置的随机性需求，实现部分去随机化。

Method: 作者利用Nguyen的二分法框架，将n个独立随机求值位置替换为固定有理函数Φ的迭代序列{x_j = Φ^j(x_0)}，其中初始点x_0在F_{p^d}^*中随机选取。技术核心是部分分式非退化性论证，利用有理迭代的不同极点来保证方案的统计安全性，从而将随机性从nd·log p比特降低到d·log p比特。

Result: 该构造在n=O(d/log_p d)且k≥2的参数范围内成立，且对单块泄漏达到完美安全性（统计距离精确为零），较Maji等人的结果在受限参数区间内有所改进。

Conclusion: 本文通过有理迭代的部分去随机化方法，在特定参数区间内显著降低了抗泄漏Shamir秘密共享的求值位置随机性开销，同时实现了更强的完美安全性保证，为后续完全去随机化或更广参数范围内的构造提供了思路。

Abstract: We make progress on the question of constructing explicit evaluation places for leakage-resilient Shamir's secret sharing, over composite order fields. Previously, Maji et al. (EUROCRYPT 2024) showed that random evaluation places yield Shamir's secret sharing over the composite order field $\mathbb{F}_{p^d}$ that is statistically secure against physical-bit leakage. Later, Nguyen (EUROCRYPT 2025) established a dichotomy that linear code-based secret-sharing scheme over the field $\mathbb{F}_{p^d}$ is either statistically secure or completely insecure against such leakage.
  Building upon Nguyen's dichotomy, we present a partial derandomization of evaluation places, improving upon the Maji et al. result for a restricted regime of parameters. We replace the random choice of $n$ independent evaluation places by the iterates $x_j = Φ^j(x_0)$ of a simple fixed rational function $Φ$, where the initial point $x_0 \in \mathbb{F}_{p^d}^*$ is randomly chosen. The randomness in the evaluation places thus drops from $nd \log p$ bits to $d\log p$ bits. Our construction is valid for the regime $n = O(d/\log_p d)$, and any reconstruction threshold $k \ge 2$; in fact, the scheme attains perfect security (statistical distance exactly zero) against single-block leakage. Our technique is a partial fraction nondegeneracy argument that exploits the distinct poles of the rational iterates.

</details>


### [56] [YazSes: An Offline, Privacy-First, Cross-Platform Hold-to-Talk Voice-Dictation System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28878)
*Mohsen Seyedkazemi Ardebili*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: YazSes是一个完全运行在设备端（on-device）的开源语音听写守护进程，支持Linux、macOS和Windows三平台。它使用faster-whisper（CPU int8）进行本地语音转录，并通过正则命令语法和可选的小语言模型路由器将语音映射为编辑器/终端操作。系统采用按压说话（hold-to-talk）模式，无遥测，可选个性化循环将语料库加密保存在本地。在LibriSpeech测试集上WER为2.59%-4.82%，解码速度快于实时（RTF 0.520），命令语法实现100%动作准确率和0%误报率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云端语音听写服务虽然准确率高，但需要将用户语音流式传输给远程提供商，这在隐私敏感行业、离线或气隙（air-gapped）环境中不可接受。现有的设备端替代方案要么受平台限制（platform-locked），要么面向专家级脚本编写而非即插即用的听写体验。需要一个完全本地化、跨平台、开箱即用的语音听写解决方案。

Method: YazSes采用分段流水线（staged pipeline）架构，通过基于协议的平台抽象层（protocol-based platform abstraction）统一三个操作系统后端，控制平面使用JSON-RPC。语音采集采用push-to-talk（按压说话）而非始终监听；转录使用faster-whisper（CPU int8量化）在本地完成；命令识别通过快速正则表达式语法实现，由可选的小语言模型路由器增强，能将语音映射到编辑器与终端操作。系统设计了隐私与威胁模型，包含加密的设备端个性化循环，只提出配置建议而不会发送数据。

Result: 在200条LibriSpeech test-clean语音（40位说话人）上，WER从tiny.en的4.82%到small.en的2.59%，small.en的实时因子为0.520（CPU无GPU解码快于实时）。命令语法在普通听写上达到100%动作准确率、0.0%误报率，单次调用耗时0.021ms；非解码流水线额外开销仅0.289ms。macOS和Windows后端已实现并通过单元测试，但未进行端到端评估。

Conclusion: YazSes展示了在不牺牲隐私的前提下实现高准确率、实时设备端语音听写的可行性。其跨平台协议抽象、可验证的隐私模型和极低的流水线开销使其适合隐私敏感场景；系统及可复现的基准测试工具已公开，为后续研究提供了基础。

Abstract: Cloud voice-dictation services deliver strong accuracy but require streaming a user's speech to a remote provider, an unacceptable trade-off in privacy-sensitive professions and offline or air-gapped settings; the leading on-device alternatives are either platform-locked or aimed at expert scripting rather than plug-and-play dictation. We present YazSes, an open-source (Apache-2.0) hold-to-talk voice dictation daemon that runs entirely on-device, with a single codebase targeting Linux, macOS, and Windows through a protocol-based platform abstraction. YazSes transcribes speech locally with faster-whisper (CPU, int8) and injects the result into the focused application; a fast regex command grammar, backed by an optional small-language-model router, maps utterances to editor and terminal actions. Nothing leaves the machine: recording is push-to-talk rather than always-listening, there is no telemetry, and an opt-in personalization loop keeps its corpus encrypted on-device and proposes configuration changes instead of shipping data out. We describe the system architecture -- a staged pipeline behind a protocol-based platform abstraction with a JSON-RPC control plane -- and its privacy and threat model. We evaluate the shipping Python implementation on a single commodity Linux laptop; the macOS and Windows backends are implemented and unit-tested but not end-to-end evaluated here. On 200 LibriSpeech test-clean utterances spanning 40 speakers, word error rate ranges from 4.82% (tiny.en) to 2.59% (small.en) at a real-time factor of 0.520 for small.en, decoding faster than real time on CPU with no GPU. The command grammar reaches 100% action accuracy with a 0.0% false-positive rate on plain dictation at 0.021 ms per call, and the non-decode pipeline adds 0.289 ms of overhead. The system and the reproducible benchmark harness behind every number in this paper are public.

</details>


### [57] [Hollow-LLM Attack: Computationally Trivial Weights in Zero-Knowledge Verification of LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28884)
*Chen Gong,Beijie Liu,Mengyuan Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一个被忽视的"努力差距"：零知识（ZK）大语言模型推理证明虽然在每个实例上保证了方程级正确性，但并未绑定生成输出所付出的计算努力。作者形式化了这一差距，并提出"空心LLM攻击"（Hollow-LLM Attack），即不诚实的服务提供商保留声明的架构和参数量，但嵌入具有代数结构的"幽灵权重"来坍缩有效计算，从而以远小于声明模型的计算成本生成可验证的正确输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM规模增长并主要经由远程平台提供服务，验证推理执行的忠实性（确保提供商确实执行了所宣称的模型和计算负载）变得至关重要。ZK推理承诺公开可验证性和每个实例的等式正确性保证，但作者发现它并未约束产生输出的实际计算努力，这构成一个被忽视的安全漏洞。

Method: 作者首先形式化定义了"努力差距"（effort gap）。然后提出Hollow-LLM攻击：不诚实的提供商保持声明的架构和参数数量不变，但嵌入"幽灵权重"（ghost weights），其代数结构使有效计算坍缩成远小于声明规模的计算。作者刻画了与标准Transformer块兼容的具体幽灵权重族，并证明这些空心部署在相同验证电路下能够以零质量损失大幅降低服务成本。

Result: 攻击者（同时也是证明者）能够生成满足验证电路并产生有效证明的见证，即使其执行的计算量仅与一个远小于声明模型的模型相当。这种空心部署在相同验证电路下显著降低服务成本且无质量损失，形成了一个有利可图的均衡。

Conclusion: 正确的推理证明并不意味着大规模模型执行的证明。这凸显了需要额外的保护措施，将正确性绑定到可验证的计算工作上。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) grow in scale and are predominantly served from remote platforms, verifying faithful inference execution becomes critical (i.e., ensuring that a provider actually executes the advertised model and computational workload rather than a tampered or downsized variant). Zero-knowledge (ZK) LLM inference offers an appealing approach. It promises public verifiability and delivers per-instance guarantees of equational correctness by proving that an output is consistent with executing a public architecture under committed, private weights. Though, we show that it does not bind the effort expended to produce the output. In this paper, we formalize this overlooked effort gap and introduce the Hollow-LLM Attack, in which a dishonest provider retains the declared architecture and parameter count but embeds ghost weights whose algebraic structure collapses effective computation. These witnesses satisfy the verification circuit and yield valid proofs, even though the dishonest model owner, who serves as the prover, performs computation commensurate with a much smaller model than the declared public architecture. This creates a profitable equilibrium in which providers deliver provably correct outputs at small-model cost while overclaiming model size. Accordingly, we characterize concrete families of ghost weights that compose with standard transformer blocks and show that such hollow deployments substantially reduce serving cost with zero quality loss under the same verification circuit. These findings underscore that proof of correct inference is not proof of large-model execution and necessitate additional protections to bind correctness to verifiable computational work.

</details>


### [58] [A Biometric Sensor Network to Enable Real-Time Measurement of Individual Student Engagement in STEM Lecture Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28944)
*Ahmed Elsayed*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种生物传感器网络（BSN），用于在STEM课堂环境中实现学生参与度的实时、无侵入测量与持续追踪，通过分布式学生处理单元（SPU）在设备端完成面部检测、视线估计和情感分析，同时严格保护数据隐私。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学生参与度是STEM教育中学术表现和留存率的关键预测因子，但现有测量方法具有侵入性强、人工负担重或不适合实时课堂应用等缺陷。

Method: 提出由学生处理单元（SPU）组成的生物传感器网络（BSN），每个SPU支持数据集采集模式和分析模式两种运行方式；分析模式下在设备端完成10秒视频片段的实时推理（面部检测、视线估计、情感分析），不存储或传输原始帧；配套安全后端基础设施负责设备认证、会话编排和加密数据摄取。

Result: 系统满足五项设计目标：非侵入性、非侵犯性、非污名化、实时性和自动化，同时确保学生数据的安全与隐私保护；完整集成了硬件设计、计算机视觉流水线、无线网络、安全协议和会话级数据管理。

Conclusion: 该BSN系统为STEM课堂中学生参与度的实时测量提供了一种兼顾伦理与隐私的无侵入式解决方案，实现了完全设备端处理以确保无可识别视频数据离开设备。

Abstract: Student engagement (SE) is a critical predictor of academic performance and retention in STEM education, yet existing measurement approaches are often intrusive, manually intensive, or unsuitable for real-time classroom use. This thesis proposes a novel $\textit{Biometric Sensor Network}$ (BSN) designed to enable real-time measurement and continuous tracking of individual student engagement in STEM classroom environments. The system enables capturing of behavioral, emotional, and cognitive indicators through camera-based sensing while preserving ethical and privacy constraints.
  To measure these indicators unobtrusively and ethically, we propose a BSN composed of $\textit{Student Processing Units}$ (SPUs) that function as distributed sensing nodes. The network is explicitly designed to satisfy five objectives: it must be $\textbf{non-intrusive}, \textbf{non-invasive}, \textbf{non-stigmatizing}, \textbf{real-time}$, and $\textbf{automatic}$, while ensuring rigorous protection of student data security and privacy.
  Each SPU supports two operational modes: (i) a $\textit{dataset-collection mode}$, in which raw student video is temporarily recorded to construct a private SE dataset for model training and validation, and (ii) an $\textit{analysis mode}$, in which the SPU performs real-time inference on 10-second video segments without storing or transmitting raw frames. In this analysis role, each SPU enables fully on-device processing---including face detection, gaze estimation, and affective analysis---ensuring that no identifiable video data leaves the device. A secure backend infrastructure manages device authentication, session orchestration, and encrypted data ingestion. The full system integrates hardware design, computer-vision pipelines, wireless networking, security protocols, and session-level data management.

</details>


### [59] [MESS: Fast and Private Semantic Search on Multi-Graph HNSW](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28999)
*Haoyu Cui,Zengpeng Li,Tien Tuan Anh Dinh,Mei Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MESS是一个实现隐私、准确性和效率三者兼顾的语义搜索系统，通过将向量映射为二进制码、应用LSH和随机化响应，并构建多图HNSW索引来保护数据、查询、访问模式和搜索模式的隐私，同时保持高性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语义搜索隐私保护方案（基于同态加密、ORAM或差分隐私）无法同时满足隐私、准确性和效率这三个目标，存在明显短板。

Method: MESS将原始向量映射为二进制码，应用局部敏感哈希（LSH）和随机化响应进行扰动，并在扰动后的编码上构建多图层次可导航小世界（HNSW）索引。采用两阶段查询扰动机制实现搜索模式隐私，多图索引缓解扰动对结果质量的影响。

Result: 实验结果证明MESS的延迟相比最先进的基线方法降低了最多15.08倍。

Conclusion: MESS通过巧妙的编码、扰动和索引设计，首次在语义搜索系统中同时实现了数据/查询/访问/搜索模式隐私、高准确率和高效率。

Abstract: Semantic search systems map data to a high-dimensional vector space and support retrieval of similar data via approximate nearest neighbor search. When the system is hosted by a trusted cloud provider, there is no privacy for the data or the query. Our goal is to design a system with three properties: privacy, accuracy, and efficiency. Existing works adopt either homomorphic encryption (HE), oblivious RAM (ORAM), or a differential privacy (DP) approach. They fall short of achieving all three properties.
  In this paper, we present MESS, a system that realizes our goal. It maps the original vectors into binary codes, applies locality-sensitive hashing (LSH) and randomized response, and constructs a multi-graph Hierarchical Navigable Small World (HNSW) index over the perturbed codes. MESS ensures data, query, and access pattern privacy. It also ensures search pattern privacy via a two-phase query perturbation mechanism. The multi-graph index mitigates the impact of perturbation on result quality, thereby achieving accuracy. MESS is efficient because search is performed directly over perturbed codes, without the overhead of homomorphic encryption or ORAM. We give formal analysis of the system's privacy and extensive evaluation of its performance. The results show that MESS achieves up to 15.08\times lower latency than state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [60] [GoldenRetriever: Non-Interactive Homomorphic Encrypted Retrieval for Privacy-Preserving RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29019)
*Yang Gao,Gang Quan,Scott Piersall,Qian Lou,Dongdong Wang,Liqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于阈值选择的非交互式加密检索框架（用于RAG），使用CKKS同态加密实现全流程加密的文档检索，将计算复杂度从二次降为线性，并通过掩码极化方法保证文档恢复精度，在保证检索效果的同时显著降低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统通常处理明文数据，存在隐私泄露风险；已有的隐私保护检索方案（如同态加密、PIR）依赖交互式协议或top-k排序机制，导致高延迟和潜在的信息泄漏，亟需一种更高效的隐私保护检索方案。

Method: 提出基于阈值选择的非交互式加密检索框架：1）用预设阈值替代top-k排序，将复杂度从O(n²)降为O(n)；2）基于CKKS同态加密实现完全加密的相似度计算和文档选择，不泄露查询内容、中间分数和选中的索引；3）引入精度稳定的掩码极化方法，弥合近似加密计算与离散token重构之间的差距。

Result: 在标准检索基准上，该方法在保持竞争力检索效果的同时，相比基于排序的加密方法显著降低了延迟。

Conclusion: 阈值选择可以作为可扩展且安全的RAG系统的实用基础，在隐私保护和检索效率之间取得了良好的平衡。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) enhances large language models by incorporating external knowledge, but existing pipelines typically operate on plaintext data, raising significant privacy concerns. Prior work on privacy-preserving retrieval leverages cryptographic techniques such as homomorphic encryption (HE) and private information retrieval (PIR), but often relies on interactive protocols or ranking-based selection mechanisms that incur high latency and potential information leakage. In this paper, we propose a practical non-interactive encrypted retrieval framework for RAG based on threshold selection. Instead of performing expensive top-$k$ ranking under encryption, our approach selects documents whose similarity scores exceed a predefined threshold, reducing computational complexity from quadratic to linear in the corpus size. We implement this design using CKKS-based homomorphic computation, enabling fully encrypted similarity evaluation and document selection without revealing query content, intermediate scores, or selected indices. To bridge the gap between approximate encrypted computation and discrete token reconstruction, we introduce a precision-stable mask polarization method that ensures accurate recovery of selected documents. Experiments on standard retrieval benchmarks demonstrate that our approach achieves competitive retrieval effectiveness while significantly reducing latency compared to ranking-based encrypted methods. These results highlight threshold-based selection as a practical foundation for scalable and secure RAG systems.

</details>


### [61] [Memory Provenance Laundering in LLM Agents: A Non-Amplification Firewall for Persistent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29167)
*Jinghan Xu,Yiyong Xiao,Wanru Shao,Hankai Liu,Xinjin Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现LLM智能体在长期记忆整合过程中存在"记忆来源洗白"漏洞：外部不可信观测可被重写为看似可信的用户历史，从而保留动作触发器的同时抹除低信任来源信息。作者提出PPMF（来源保持记忆防火墙），一种轻量级记忆中间件，通过保留平台维护的来源信息并将工具调用的动作风险与记忆来源权威度匹配来实现防护。评估显示受损记忆攻击成功率可达100%，而PPMF可拦截所有未授权高风险动作，同时保持确认后的良性动作和低风险记忆使用可执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期记忆让LLM智能体复用偏好和工作流，但未经可信来源约束的记忆会把不可信观测转化为持久的动作上下文。现有提示过滤器、内容消毒器和工具防护均无法在有损记忆整合后强制来源权威不放大，存在安全边界缺失。

Method: 提出PPMF（Provenance-Preserving Memory Firewall），一种轻量级记忆中间件：保留平台维护的来源信息，并通过将动作风险与动作相关记忆的权威度进行匹配来授权工具调用，从而实现来源权威的非放大约束。

Result: 在基于schema的评估中，易受损的整合记忆攻击成功率可达1.000；在平台来源信息、确认和风险标签完整保留时，所有未授权的的高风险动作均无法通过PPMF门控，而确认的良性动作和定向的低风险记忆使用仍可执行。

Conclusion: PPMF证明通过保持记忆来源信息并建立"动作风险-来源权威"匹配机制，可以有效封闭LLM智能体记忆整合过程中的来源洗白安全边界，兼顾安全性与可用性。

Abstract: Long-term memory lets large language model(LLM) agents reuse prior preferences and work flows, but it also turns untrusted observations into persistent action context. We identify memory provenance laundering: during LLM-based memory consolidation, an external observation may be rewritten as apparent user history or workflow support, preserving an action trigger while erasing the low-trust source that should limit its authority. Existing prompt filters, content sanitizers, and tool guards do not enforce source-authority non-amplification after lossy memory consolidation. We formalize this boundary and instantiate it as Provenance-Preserving Memory Fire wall (PPMF), a lightweight memory middleware that preserves platform-maintained provenance and authorizes tool calls by matching action risk to the authority of action-relevant memories. In our schema-grounded evaluation with fixed risk policies, vulnerable consolidated memories reach up to 1.000 attack success rate(ASR); with intact platform-maintained provenance, confirmation, and risk labels, no evaluated unauthorized high-risk action passes the PPMF gate while confirmed benign actions and targeted low-risk memory use remain executable.

</details>


### [62] [Alignment Is Local: A Paired Diagnostic for GUI Agents under User-Side Persuasion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29199)
*Haoxin An,Yunpeng Song,Zihao Bai,Zhongmin Cai,Guojun Xiong,Chenhao Lin,Wentao Chen,Feng Wei,Chao Shen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文论证了提示级对齐（prompt-level alignment）在GUI智能体安全部署中的局限性：它在单轮、显式意图的评估切片中表现良好，但在多轮动态交互中会系统性失效。通过对三个前沿GUI智能体的配对诊断实验，发现一行护栏指令可将单轮攻击成功率（ASR）降低约40个百分点，但在四轮升级式交互中，受护栏保护的ASR回升约20个百分点；且护栏主要对显式请求生效，对隐蔽请求的防御效果有限，因此静态单轮ASR会系统性地高估实际部署鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前移动端GUI智能体的主流轻量防御手段是提示级对齐，但这类防御是否能在真实动态交互中保持有效尚不明确。作者主张提示级对齐是一种"局部现象"，仅在狭窄的评估切片（单轮、明确语言表达的意图）中可靠，而真实用户可经由多轮对话、隐性意图等路径绕过，因此需要更严谨的动态与威胁条件下的对齐评估。

Method: 作者设计了配对诊断（paired diagnostic）方法，在三个前沿GUI智能体上开展实验。实验不依赖环境注入，而是采用屏幕接地（screen-grounded）、用户侧说服（user-side persuasion）的攻击范式，从独立单轮探测扩展到四轮升级式（escalation chain）交互，同时比较护栏存在与否、显式与隐蔽（隐式表达）请求两种条件下的攻击成功率（ASR）与过度拒绝（over-refusal）比率。
