Result: 实验结果显示：一行护栏指令在独立单轮探测中可将ASR降低约40个百分点，且过度拒绝代价接近零；但转移到四轮升级式交互后，受护栏保护的ASR在每款模型上回升约20个百分点。相比中性基线，该回升对Qwen而言体现为护栏侵蚀，对Claude和GPT则主要由与防御正交的动态风险驱动。护栏的存在使显著性差距（salience gap）的符号反转：无护栏时隐蔽请求并非系统性更成功，有护栏时反而更成功，说明防御主要针对明确命名的意图而起效。

Conclusion: 结果表明，静态单轮ASR会以一个系统且可预测的幅度高估GUI智能体的实际部署鲁棒性。提示级对齐作为局部防御无法覆盖动态交互的威胁空间，需要在真实交互条件下重新设计对齐与评估框架。

Abstract: Trustworthy deployment of GUI agents in ubiquitous computing settings requires alignment that survives dynamic interaction and precise threat conditions, not just single-turn refusal of explicit harmful requests. We argue that prompt-level alignment, the dominant lightweight defense in current mobile agents, is a local phenomenon: it works reliably only in the narrow evaluation slice where it is typically measured, namely single-turn, explicitly-verbalized intent, and degrades systematically along two axes that any real user can traverse. Using a paired diagnostic on three frontier GUI agents, screen-grounded, user-side persuasion, with no environment injection, we show that a one-line guardrail achieves large single-shot ASR reductions, up to roughly 40 points, at near-zero over-refusal cost. Nevertheless, moving from independent probes to four-turn escalation chains raises guarded ASR by approximately 20 points on every model. Relative to the neutral baselines, this increase reflects substantial guardrail erosion for Qwen but a largely defense-orthogonal dynamic risk for Claude and GPT. The sign of the salience gap flips under the guardrail: concealed requests are not systematically more successful than explicit ones without a guardrail, but are more successful with one, indicating that the defense engages primarily when intent is named. Static single-turn ASR therefore overstates deployed robustness by a systematic and predictable margin.

</details>


### [63] [MOSAIC: Masked Outsourcing of Secure AI Computations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29221)
*James Hsin-yu Chiang,Sheila Zingg,Kari Kostiainen,Srdjan Capkun*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MOSAIC是一种安全外包AI计算的新协议，通过创新的矩阵乘法掩蔽协议，使计算能力弱的可信客户端能够将大规模AI计算（如70B参数的Transformer推理）外包给不可信的服务器，同时保证客户端持有输入和模型且服务器两者都学不到。它通过引入少量噪声放松正确性，实现了最优的渐进客户端开销，并将安全性归约到decisional LWE和LPN假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可信但计算能力弱的客户端需要安全地将现代AI工作负载（如大规模Transformer推理）外包给强大的不可信服务器，同时确保服务器无法学习输入或模型。现有的矩阵乘法掩蔽协议无法扩展到较大的矩阵规模，难以支撑现代大规模AI工作负载。

Method: 提出MOSAIC协议，核心是一种新型矩阵乘法掩蔽协议，通过向乘法结果引入少量噪声放松正确性，实现最优渐进客户端开销。针对噪声在Transformer多层中累积的问题，设计了一种基于随机Hadamard旋转的误差缩放机制来约束误差增长。安全性归约到decisional LWE和LPN假设。同时提出了端到端实现方案，通过保持小型可信计算基（TCB），将大部分AI计算外包给不可信加速器。

Result: MOSAIC的矩阵乘法掩蔽协议可扩展到远大于先前工作的矩阵规模；具体运行时间比先前工作快数个数量级。在70B参数的Transformer模型上，其困惑度与主流量化方法相当，在HumanEval上甚至匹配全精度BF16推理性能。

Conclusion: MOSAIC展示了通过保持小型TCB并将大部分AI计算外包给不可信加速器，可以实现大规模机密AI计算的可扩展路径，适用于现代数据中心中分布式、异构硬件上的机密推理场景。

Abstract: We address the challenge of securely and efficiently outsourcing AI computations from a trusted but computationally weak client to an untrusted but powerful server, in the setting where the client holds both the input and the model, and the server must learn neither. We present MOSAIC, whose core is a novel matrix-multiplication masking protocol that scales to far larger matrices than prior work, enabling the safe outsourcing of modern workloads such as large transformer inference. By introducing small amounts of noise to the multiplication result and thereby relaxing correctness, MOSAIC achieves optimal asymptotic client overhead and concrete runtimes orders of magnitude faster than prior work. Its security reduces to the decisional LWE and LPN assumptions.
  Because this noise accumulates across the many layers of a transformer, a key technical challenge is bounding error growth; MOSAIC addresses this with an error-scaling mechanism based on random Hadamard rotations. On large 70B transformer models, MOSAIC's perplexity is comparable to popular quantization approaches and even matches full-precision BF16 inference on HumanEval.
  Finally, we present an end-to-end implementation showing how ideas like MOSAIC can promise a path towards large-scale confidential AI in modern data centers. Non-confidential inference is already distributed across phase (prefill/decode), layer, and time to maximize utilization of heterogeneous hardware, using RDMA-like networking to move activations, cached KV values, and weights across nodes. MOSAIC enables scaling of confidential compute by keeping the trusted computing base (TCB) small and outsourcing the bulk of the AI computation to untrusted accelerators.

</details>


### [64] [JUNO: Aggregated Vector Consensus for Optimal Asynchronous Common Subset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29244)
*Liangrong Zhao,Qin Wang,Joseph K. Liu,Jiangshan Yu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的异步网络向量共识原语——聚合向量共识（aggregated vector consensus），并基于此实现了Juno异步公共子集（ACS）协议，达到最优O(n²)消息复杂度。实验表明，相比HoneyBadgerBFT和Dory，Juno的平均吞吐量分别提升了93%和47%，填补了向量共识在完全异步网络中应用的研究空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有向量共识协议主要适用于同步或部分同步网络，在完全异步网络中的应用存在空白。作者旨在设计一种适用于异步网络的向量共识原语，并以此构建高效的ACS协议，以提升异步共识系统的性能。

Method: 作者首先提出聚合向量共识（aggregated vector consensus）这一新的原语，其核心思想是通过输出一个聚合了各独立进程输入的向量来达成共识。随后基于该原语设计并实现了Juno协议，它是一个完整的异步公共子集（ACS）协议，实现了最优的O(n²)消息复杂度。

Result: 通过实验评估，Juno相比HoneyBadgerBFT实现了93%的平均吞吐量提升，相比Dory实现了47%的平均吞吐量提升，证明了其有效性和高效性。

Conclusion: Juno协议在完全异步网络中成功实现了高效向量共识，显著提升了吞吐量性能，填补了向量共识协议在异步网络中的应用空白，为异步共识系统提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose \textit{aggregated vector consensus}, a new vector consensus primitive designed for asynchronous networks. The primitive achieves agreement by outputting a vector of values aggregated from independent process inputs. We then introduce \textsc{Juno}, an asynchronous common subset (ACS) protocol that fully implements our aggregated vector consensus to attain optimal $\mathcal{O}(n^2)$ message complexity.
  We further implement and evaluate \textsc{Juno} in comparison with the legacy HoneyBadgerBFT and the state-of-the-art Dory. Experiment results demonstrate its efficacy and efficiency. Our protocol demonstrates an average throughput performance improvement of 93\% compared with HoneyBadgerBFT and a 47\% improvement compared with Dory. Notably, our study makes significant progress in addressing the gap in applying vector consensus protocol in fully asynchronous networks.

</details>


### [65] [On the Resilience of 5G NR Against Jamming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29384)
*Sotiris Michaelides,Fidelius Lula,Daniel Eguiguren Chavez,Michael Rademacher,Martin Henze*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文在ns-3仿真器中实现并开源了一个5G干扰机，通过可控的确定性仿真，系统比较了蜂窝干扰在不同技术、频率、子载波间隔和信道带宽等维度下的影响。结果表明信道带宽和频率范围对干扰韧性起关键作用，而蜂窝技术和子载波间隔影响较小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着5G网络在工业网络和关键基础设施等可用性关键系统中的广泛应用，全面理解其对蜂窝干扰的韧性至关重要。然而现有研究仅在固定的5G物理层配置下进行孤立评估，难以进行可靠的对比分析（如比较不同频段或信道带宽在干扰韧性方面的差异），因此需要一个统一的开放平台来系统性地比较不同维度下的干扰影响。

Method: 作者在ns-3仿真器中实现并开源了一个5G干扰机，利用仿真环境的可控确定性，从多个维度对比蜂窝干扰的影响，包括蜂窝技术（cellular technologies）、频率（frequencies）、子载波间隔（subcarrier spacing）和信道带宽（channel bandwidth）。

Result: 研究结果表明，蜂窝技术和子载波间隔对干扰韧性的影响较小，而信道带宽和频率范围（frequency range）在提升干扰韧性方面起关键作用。

Conclusion: 通过开源的ns-3 5G干扰机仿真工具，本文揭示了影响5G网络干扰韧性的关键因素，指出信道带宽和频率范围是提升抗干扰能力的主要设计维度，为5G关键基础设施的干扰韧性评估提供了系统性方法和工具。

Abstract: With the increasing use of 5G networks in availability-critical systems, including industrial networks and critical infrastructure, a comprehensive understanding of their resilience to cellular jamming has become imperative. However, research so far has focused on isolated evaluations under fixed 5G physical-layer configurations, making it difficult to perform sound comparisons, for example, to identify differences between frequency bands or channel bandwidths w.r.t. jamming resilience. To fill this gap, we implement and release an open-source 5G jammer within the ns-3 simulator. Leveraging the controllable determinism of simulation, we compare the impact of cellular jamming across different dimensions such as cellular technologies, frequencies, subcarrier spacing, and channel bandwidth. Our results show that while cellular technology and subcarrier spacing have little impact, channel bandwidth and frequency range play a critical role in improving jamming resilience.

</details>


### [66] [On fair and realistic performance evaluations for graph-based lateral movement detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29390)
*Corentin Larroche*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文调查了横向移动检测研究中两个流行基准数据集在前处理和标注方法上的差异，指出这些差异严重影响下游评估的公平性和真实性，并提出合理的策略重新评估三种广泛引用的检测方法，结果与原论文显著不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 横向移动检测研究虽已取得显著进展，但文献中对基准数据集的前处理和标注方法存在大量不一致，导致评估结果缺乏公平性和可复现性，亟需系统性地审视并规范这些实践。

Method: 作者对两个流行数据集的预处理和标注方法进行了系统性调查，比较了不同论文间的做法差异及其对评估公平性和真实性的影响；随后提出了有理有据的预处理和标注策略，并在这些新策略下重新评估了三种被广泛引用的横向移动检测方法。

Result: 重新评估的结果与原论文报告的结果存在显著差异，进一步证明了数据集预处理和标注实践对横向移动检测器评估的至关重要性。

Conclusion: 数据集的前处理与标注方法对横向移动检测器的评估结果有决定性影响，研究社区需要统一和规范这些实践以保障评估的公平性与可比性。

Abstract: Research on lateral movement detection has made significant progress in recent years, spurred by the widespread availability of benchmark datasets that make evaluating detectors practical. However, the exact way in which these benchmark datasets are used varies across the literature: both the preprocessing applied before feeding the data to the detector and the labeling of lateral movement-related events change substantially from one paper to another. We survey preprocessing and labeling methodologies for two popular datasets and demonstrate their impact on the fairness and realism of downstream evaluations. We also propose well-grounded preprocessing and labeling policies for these datasets. Finally, we re-evaluate three widely cited lateral movement detection methods under these new policies; our results differ significantly from those reported in the original papers, further highlighting the critical importance of dataset preprocessing and labeling practices in evaluating lateral movement detectors.

</details>


### [67] [Enforcing Cryptographic Distributed-VCS Access Control with No Trust on Servers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29417)
*Xin Xu,Zhen Yang,Quanwei Cai,Jingqiang Lin,Liangqin Ren,Bo Chen,Yongfeng Huang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出DVAC，一种基于属性加密（ABE）和属性签名（ABS）的分布式版本控制系统访问控制方案，无需中央服务器即可实现文件粒度的读写分离访问控制，并通过以太坊智能合约维护访问控制策略，且可与Git无缝集成的原型已实现并验证了较低的额外开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VCS访问控制方案依赖中央服务器，无法在完全分布式场景下部署；同时缺乏细粒度的写权限控制，这在分布式协作环境中至关重要。

Method: DVAC采用ABE和ABS实现密码学访问控制；设计版本控制适配协议确保核心功能完整性；使用以太坊智能合约维护访问控制策略以实现分布式存储和可信管理；架构设计可无缝集成现有DVCS（如Git）。

Result: 实现了DVAC原型并集成到Git中，通过全面的性能评估证明了引入的开销是适度的（modest）。

Conclusion: DVAC能够在无中央服务器支持的分布式场景下实现文件粒度的读写分离细粒度访问控制，同时保证数据安全和版本控制功能的完整性，且与现有DVCS兼容并开销可控。

Abstract: Version control systems (VCS), including central VCS (CVCS) and distributed VCS (DVCS), are widely adopted to manage changes to software code and various types of documents. Unlike CVCS, where entities obtain data from a central server, each entity in DVCS stores the entire repository and shares it independently. In VCS, existing access control schemes require the participation of a central server and cannot be deployed in a completely distributed scenario. Additionally, these schemes often fail to enforce fine-grained access control for write permissions, which is crucial for collaborative work in a distributed environment. In this paper, we propose a distributed version control system access control scheme (named DVAC), which enforces cryptographic access control on distributed user nodes based on attribute-based encryption (ABE) and attribute-based signature (ABS). DVAC is designed to enforce a cryptographic access control protocol for DVCS, which enables file granularity read and write separation access control without the support of a central server. To ensure the integrity of the core version control functions in DVCS while protecting data security, DVAC incorporates a version control adaptation protocol. Additionally, DVAC leverages Ethereum smart contracts to maintain access control policies, ensuring distributed storage and trusted management of access policies. The architecture of DVAC is designed to seamlessly integrate with existing mature DVCS, such as Git, with minimal modifications. We have implemented a prototype of DVAC and integrated it with Git. A comprehensive performance evaluation was conducted to assess the overhead introduced by DVAC, and it was demonstrated that the overhead is modest.

</details>


### [68] [Bending the Curve: Operational Cyber Epidemiology for Ransomware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29444)
*Stephen V Flowerday,Nikolay Lipskiy,Steven Furnell,Callum E Flowerday,John Hale*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种操作型网络流行病学框架，将SEIR流行病模型适配到勒索软件事件管理中，用基本/有效再生数（R0、Re）作为SOC近实时决策辅助，并通过公开事件案例和实用保护阈值启发式帮助将Re降至1以下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 勒索软件常被视为检测问题，但最具破坏性的事件更像疫情爆发——单一立足点可通过身份、管理工具和共享服务扩散，而响应者在信息不完整的情况下做出关键时限决策，缺乏将技术遥测与遏制决策连接起来的共享语言。

Method: 将SEIR模型适配为勒索软件事件管理本体：Exposed表示潜伏妥协和暂存（含二次妥协前的驻留期），Infectious表示主动横向传播；借鉴ISO 5477:2023公共卫生应急准备与响应信息管理指南及2025 UNDRR-ISC危害信息档案，定义可互操作的勒索软件病例定义和跨事件比较所需的关键信息要素（EEI）；将传播状态与观测状态分离以避免混淆扩散动态与检测能力；推导将Re降至1以下的实用保护阈值启发式，并提供与工具无关的剧本卡片。

Result: 通过WannaCry、NotPetya、SolarWinds、MGM和Caesars等公开事件案例，展示了爆发式度量如何支持更早隔离、凭证遏制和恢复排序；提出了面向SOC的R0和Re方向性近实时决策辅助工具，以及实际保护阈值启发式。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是建立一种共享语言，在资源约束下将技术遥测与遏制决策相连接，帮助安全运营中心更早采取隔离和遏制行动。

Abstract: Ransomware is often treated as a detection problem, but the most disruptive incidents behave more like outbreaks. A single foothold can spread through identities, administrative tools, and shared services while responders make time-critical decisions with incomplete visibility. This paper presents an operational cyber epidemiology framework that adapts the Susceptible-Exposed-Infectious-Removed (SEIR) model to ransomware incident management. In this ontology, Exposed denotes latent compromise and staging, including the dwell period before confirmed secondary compromise, while Infectious denotes active lateral propagation. Drawing on ISO 5477:2023 guidance for public health emergency preparedness and response information management and the 2025 UNDRR-ISC Hazard Information Profiles, the framework defines interoperable ransomware case definitions and Essential Elements of Information for cross-incident comparison. Basic and effective reproduction numbers, R0 and Re, are used as directional, near-real-time decision aids for security operations centers. Propagation state is separated from observation status to avoid confusing spread dynamics with detection capability. Publicly reported incidents, including WannaCry, NotPetya, SolarWinds, and MGM and Caesars, illustrate how outbreak-style measures can support earlier isolation, credential containment, and restoration sequencing. The paper also derives practical protection-threshold heuristics aimed at reducing Re below 1 and provides a tool-agnostic playbook card linking operational information to explicit action triggers. The primary contribution is a shared language that connects technical telemetry to containment decisions under resource constraints.

</details>


### [69] [Beyond Resilience: Antifragility in Critical Infrastructure Cybersecurity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29550)
*Stephen Flowerday,Mauricio Papa,Ethan Flowerday*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了面向关键基础设施（CI）网络安全的反脆弱性理论（AFT），以五态弹性系统模型和基于Jensen收益与灾后收益的有界数学定义为框架，通过CISSM网络事件数据库与HAI硬件在环工控数据集的二层实证设计，验证了OT相关行业脆弱性负担更高（65.3% vs 46.8%）且攻击引发的过程状态偏移可观测（攻击样本超基线95分位数的概率为正常样本的7.43倍），并为未来反脆弱性测试确立了差异化脆弱性负担和过程级扰动可观测性两个前提条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关键基础设施网络安全需要超越恢复、走向扰动下有界改进的框架，但针对运营技术的经验性理论仍然有限，亟需建立实证支撑的理论基础。

Method: 提出AFT理论，包含五态弹性系统模型和基于Jensen增益与灾后增益的有界数学定义；采用两层实证设计，结合CISSM网络事件数据库的CI相关子集与HAI硬件在环工控数据集，检验三个确认性假设和一个探索性命题。

Result: OT邻近行业的破坏性或混合事件占比显著高于对照行业（65.3% vs 46.8%，p<0.001），且物理攻击和数据攻击子类型更集中；HAI中攻击标签观测超出基线偏差95百分位的概率是正常观测的7.43倍（p<0.001）；连续攻击窗口内过程状态偏差显著下降（Spearman rho=-0.688，p=0.007），表明存在可测量的响应变异。

Conclusion: 研究结果确立了未来反脆弱性测试的两个前提条件：差异化脆弱性负担和过程级扰动可观测性，为CI网络安全领域的反脆弱性实证研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Critical infrastructure cybersecurity increasingly requires frameworks that move beyond recovery toward bounded improvement under disruption, yet empirically grounded theories for operational technology remain limited. This paper develops a Theory of Antifragility (AFT) for critical infrastructure (CI) cybersecurity, anchored in a five-state Resilient System Model and a bounded mathematical definition based on Jensen gain and post-disruption gain. A two-layer empirical design pairs a CI-relevant subset of the CISSM Cyber Events Database with the HAI hardware-in-the-loop industrial control dataset and tests three confirmatory hypotheses and one exploratory proposition. OT-adjacent sectors show significantly higher shares of disruptive or mixed events than comparison sectors (65.3 percent versus 46.8 percent, p less than 0.001), together with a greater concentration of physical-attack and data-attack subtypes. In HAI, attack-labeled observations were 7.43 times more likely than normal observations to exceed the 95th percentile of baseline deviation (p less than 0.001). Across successive attack windows, mean process-state deviation declined significantly (Spearman rho = -0.688, p = 0.007), indicating measurable response variation rather than proof of adaptive gain. Together, the findings establish two prerequisites for future antifragility testing: differentiated fragility burden and process-level perturbation observability.

</details>


### [70] [CWEEP: A Lexical Static Analysis Framework for CWE Early Prevention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29604)
*Bryan Kwan,Benjamin Tan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CWEEP是一个用于检测寄存器传输级（RTL）代码中安全弱点的静态分析框架。它不需要详细的安全规范，可以在RTL开发早期使用；能够定位漏洞在RTL中的确切位置，并在适用时提供自动代码修复建议。在包含3874个有缺陷模块的数据集上，CWEEP的正确警告率为60.8%，远高于先前工作工具的17.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着硬件层成为攻击者的关注焦点，改进硬件安全验证技术的需求日益迫切。现有最先进的安全验证技术需要具有安全专业知识的人员投入大量手动工作，且缺乏标准方法来定位RTL代码中故障的确切位置。

Method: CWEEP采用静态分析框架，无需详细的安全规范即可运行，使其能在RTL开发早期阶段使用。该框架能够识别RTL中潜在漏洞的确切位置，并支持自动代码修复建议。

Result: 使用文献中的数据集，在两组手动插入缺陷的SoC设计以及一个包含3874个由大语言模型生成的有缺陷模块的数据集上进行评估。CWEEP的正确警告率达到60.8%，而先前工作工具的相同数据集上正确警告率仅为17.5%。

Conclusion: CWEEP作为一种无需安全规范的静态分析框架，在硬件安全验证方面显著优于先前的工作，能在早期RTL开发阶段有效检测安全弱点并提供修复建议。

Abstract: As the hardware layer becomes a focus point for attackers, the need for improved hardware security verification techniques is more important than ever. State-of-the-art security verification techniques require significant manual effort from individuals with security expertise. Furthermore, there is no standard method to locate where the fault lies within the register transfer level (RTL) code. This paper presents CWEEP, a static analysis framework for detecting security weaknesses in RTL. CWEEP does not require a detailed security specification, so it can be used in the early stages of RTL development while properties are still under construction. Furthermore, CWEEP can identify the exact location in the RTL where the potential vulnerability resides and supports automatic code repair suggestions when applicable. Using datasets from the literature, we evaluate the performance of CWEEP on a set of two SoC designs with manually inserted bugs and on a large language model generated dataset, consisting of 3874 buggy modules. We find that CWEEP issues a correct warning up to 60.8% of the time. In contrast, the tool from a previous work issued a correct warning 17.5% of the time for the same dataset.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [71] [OpenClaw and Ollama in Agentic AI: Toward Fully Autonomous and Scalable AI Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28629)
*Konstantinos I. Roumeliotis,Ranjan Sapkota*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了面向Agentic AI的分层架构框架，通过OpenClaw-Ollama全栈系统的原型验证，证明持久记忆、工具调用和自适应决策等能力源于系统级集成而非独立模型，并探讨了可扩展性、安全、隐私、治理和评估等挑战，为构建可扩展、安全、可信的自主智能体系统提供了统一架构基础和路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从被动式LLM向持久化、可执行系统的快速转变暴露了Agentic AI在架构理解上的关键空白，特别是推理层、编排层和执行层的分离问题。尽管已有进展，但用于设计和评估全栈智能体系统的统一框架仍然有限，亟需一个综合性的分层架构来填补这一空白。

Method: 本文提出了Agentic AI的综合分层架构，描述了从被动LLM接口到具有记忆、规划和持续执行的持久化目标驱动自主智能体的演进过程。具体以OpenClaw和Ollama作为全栈Agentic AI系统进行分析——其中Ollama负责LLM推理层，OpenClaw负责智能体运行时编排（整合推理、工具使用和动作执行），并通过原型实验进行验证。

Result: 原型实验验证表明：持久记忆、工具利用和自适应决策等能力从系统级集成中涌现而非由独立模型产生，且性能随着架构复杂性的增加而持续提升。研究同时识别了可扩展性、安全、隐私、治理和评估等方面的挑战，强调需要稳健的基准测试和系统级设计。

Conclusion: 该工作为Agentic AI建立了统一的架构基础，验证了全栈自主AI智能体的有效性，并提出了面向可扩展多智能体架构、分布式自主系统和以人为本的Agentic AI框架的未来方向，以支持负责任的部署。所有模型、代码和数据集已公开发布以支持复现和基准测试。

Abstract: The rapid transition from reactive large language models (LLMs) to persistent, action-capable systems has exposed critical gaps in the architectural understanding of Agentic AI, particularly in separating inference, orchestration, and execution layers for autonomous AI agents. Despite recent advances, unified frameworks for designing and evaluating full-stack agentic systems remain limited. This paper presents a comprehensive, layered architecture for Agentic AI, outlining the evolution from reactive LLM interfaces to persistent, goal-driven autonomous AI agents with memory, planning, and continuous execution. We analyze OpenClaw and Ollama as a full-stack Agentic AI system, where Ollama serves as the LLM inference layer and OpenClaw enables agent runtime orchestration, integrating reasoning, tool use, and action execution. A prototype experimental validation of the OpenClaw-Ollama architecture demonstrates that capabilities such as persistent memory, tool utilization, and adaptive decision-making emerge from system-level integration rather than standalone models, with performance improving consistently as architectural complexity increases. The study further examines challenges in scalability, security, privacy, governance, and evaluation of agentic systems, highlighting the need for robust benchmarking and system-level design. Future directions include scalable multi-agent architectures, distributed autonomous systems, and human-aware Agentic AI frameworks for responsible deployment. Overall, this work establishes a unified architectural foundation for Agentic AI, validates the effectiveness of full-stack autonomous AI agents, and provides a roadmap for building scalable, secure, and trustworthy agentic systems. All models, code, and datasets are publicly released to support reproducibility and benchmarking.

</details>


### [72] [Can AI Evaluate AI Scientists? A Benchmarking Study of Autonomous Research Generation Systems Using Automated Multi-Model Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28631)
*Vaibhava Lakshmi Ravideshik,Mayank Kejriwal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出并实现了一个基于自动化同行评审的AI科学家系统基准测试协议，利用多个前沿大语言模型（GPT-5.4、Gemini、Claude）从原创性、科学严谨性、清晰度和重要性四个维度评估论文质量。通过对Sakana AI (v1 & v2)、CycleResearcher、Data-to-Paper和FARS的60+15篇论文进行评估，发现FARS基准论文显著优于其他系统（平均得分2.14-2.47 vs 1.00-1.87），且Gemini和Claude评审高度一致（ρ=0.907），验证了多模型LLM评估的可靠性和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI Scientist系统具有加速科学发现的潜力，但如何评估和比较AI生成论文的质量仍然是一个开放挑战。此前缺乏定量基准来公平比较不同的自主研究框架，因此需要建立严格的评估协议。

Method: 采用自动化同行评审系统，利用三个前沿LLM评审者（GPT-5.4、Gemini、Claude）从四个核心维度（原创性、科学严谨性、清晰度、重要性）评估论文。四个AI Scientist框架（Sakana AI v1&v2、CycleResearcher、Data-to-Paper）分别在15个一致的FARS研究提案上运行，生成60篇论文，与15篇FARS基准论文一并评估。

Result: FARS基准论文在平均得分上显著超越所有竞争框架（2.14-2.47 vs 1.00-1.87），在Gemini和Claude评估中得分是次优系统的2倍以上。Gemini与Claude评审高度一致（ρ=0.907, p<0.001），且与综合得分极强相关（ρ=0.961）。GPT-5.4与其他评审者一致性较弱（ρ≈0.32），表明其使用不同的评估标准。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了首个AI Scientist系统的定量基准，证明多模型LLM自动化评估为评估自主研究质量提供了可扩展且一致的框架。然而GPT-5.4的偏差也提示需要多模型交叉验证以确保评估的全面性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: AI Scientist systems capable of autonomous research have the potential to significantly accelerate scientific discovery. However, evaluating and comparing the quality of AI-generated papers remains an open challenge. We propose and implement a rigorous benchmarking protocol using an automated peer-review system that harnesses frontier large language models to assess scientific papers across four core dimensions: originality, scientific rigor, clarity, and significance. We evaluate four leading AI Scientist frameworks: \textit{Sakana AI (v1 & v2)}, \textit{CycleResearcher}, and \textit{Data-to-Paper}. Each framework was run on a consistent set of 15 research proposals published by a commercial autonomous AI scientist company (FARS), generating 60 papers that we evaluate alongside 15 FARS benchmark papers. Using three independent LLM reviewers (GPT-5.4, Gemini, and Claude), we find that FARS benchmark papers significantly outperform all competing frameworks, achieving mean scores of 2.14--2.47 on a 1--5 scale compared to 1.00--1.87 for other systems. Notably, FARS scores are more than 2$\times$ higher than the next-best systems on Gemini and Claude evaluations. We find strong agreement among Gemini and Claude ($ρ$ = 0.907, $p < 0.001$), and both correlate extremely strongly with the synthesis score ($ρ$ = 0.961, $p < 0.001$), validating the reliability of automated evaluation. However, GPT-5.4 exhibits weaker agreement ($ρ\approx 0.32$), suggesting it evaluates papers using different criteria. These results establish the first quantitative benchmark for AI Scientist systems and demonstrate that multi-model LLM evaluation provides a scalable, consistent framework for assessing autonomous research quality.

</details>


### [73] [LLM Framework for Discovering Major Mathematical Conjectures: AI's Quest for the Next Riemann Hypothesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28632)
*Alizer Wong,Zixin Zeng,Yi Tan,Wenyuan Li,Xuhang Chen,Xingru Lai,Yang Shi,Liangsi Lu,Yanhui Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个三阶段流水线用于重大数学猜想发现：区域搜索、反思性验证和Lean 4/Mathlib形式化验证。在20个候选猜想上测试，全部通过Lean解析和类型检查，且无重复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重大数学猜想仍严重依赖专家直觉，缺乏统一的方法来系统性地生成和验证具有重大数学潜力的猜想。

Method: 提出三阶段流水线：(1) 区域搜索——从显式局部证据模块出发寻找候选区域；(2) 反思性验证——评估候选猜想的基础性、新颖性和潜在重要性；(3) 在Lean 4和Mathlib中进行形式化验证。

Result: 在20个候选猜想上实验：20/20通过Lean解析和类型检查，20/20未被exact?直接吸收，20/20未被aesop自动证明，且无显式重复或近似重复。

Conclusion: 该流水线展示了从自然语言到形式化检查的稳定通道，能够发现具有高"问题品味"（即证明可能重组研究领域语言并为人类数学研究提供持久帮助）的数学问题。

Abstract: Major mathematical conjectures still depend heavily on expert intuition, so a unified method for the systematic generation and validation of conjectures with substantial mathematical potential remains unavailable. We present a three stage pipeline for major conjecture discovery, with region search from explicit local evidence modules, reflective validation for foundationality, novelty, and potential significance, and formal validation in Lean 4 and Mathlib. The objective is the discovery of mathematical problems with high problem taste, namely problems whose proofs could reorganize the language of a research area and provide durable help to human mathematical research. Experiments on twenty candidates showstable passage from natural language to formal checks, with twenty out of twenty candidates passing Lean parsing and type checking, twenty out of twenty candidates not directly absorbed by exact?,twenty out of twenty candidates not automatically discharged by aesop, and no explicit duplicates or near duplicates.

</details>


### [74] [ThinkReset: Learnable Intermediate Interface Construction for Bounded-Context Long-Horizon Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28642)
*Fei Ding,Yongkang Zhang,Runhao Liu,Yuhao Liao,Zijian Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ThinkReset框架，指出在受限上下文窗口下长链思维推理的核心瓶颈是缺乏可复用的中间接口，而非轨迹压缩或测试时控制。通过接口写回与重置机制，并直接优化重置后继续推理的成功率，在多个长程推理基准上一致提升了固定上下文窗口下的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长链思维推理虽能提升复杂问题性能，但带来冗余累积、上下文溢出和错误锚定等问题。作者论证在有限上下文窗口下，核心瓶颈不是轨迹压缩或测试时控制，而是缺少可复用的中间接口来替代被丢弃的历史并支持持续求解。此外，识别出结果奖励驱动的长链强化学习的关键失败模式：当模型在窗口接近耗尽前尚未解决问题时，最终答案奖励会鼓励过早猜测而非继续谨慎推理。

Method: 提出ThinkReset，一种文本空间实现。该方法通过接口写回（interface writeback）和重置（reset）显式构建可复用的中间接口，并直接优化重置后的继续推理成功率。具体而言，模型在上下文快耗尽时将关键中间状态写入接口并重置上下文，使得后续推理可以基于该接口继续而非从头开始。

Result: 在多个长程推理基准上，ThinkReset在固定上下文窗口条件下一致地提升了任务成功率，验证了可复用中间接口视角的有效性。

Conclusion: 长链推理的核心瓶颈在于缺乏可复用的中间接口，而非轨迹压缩或测试时控制。通过显式构建中间接口并优化重置后继续推理的能力，可以在有限上下文下显著提升长程推理表现。

Abstract: Long chain-of-thought reasoning improves performance on complex problems, but it also introduces redundancy accumulation, context overflow, and error anchoring. We argue that under bounded context windows, the core bottleneck is not trajectory compression or test-time control, but the absence of a reusable intermediate interface that can replace discarded history and support continued solving. We further identify a key failure mode of outcome-reward-driven long-chain reinforcement learning: when the model has not solved the task before the window is nearly exhausted, the final-answer reward encourages premature guessing rather than continued careful reasoning. We propose ThinkReset, a text-space instantiation of this view. ThinkReset explicitly constructs reusable intermediate interfaces through interface writeback and reset, and directly optimizes post-reset continuation success. Across multiple long-horizon reasoning benchmarks, this perspective consistently improves success rates under fixed context windows.

</details>


### [75] [TAPR: Enhancing LLM Performance with a Task-Aware Prompt Rewriter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28657)
*Oliver Savolainen,Emanuele Bastianelli,Hosein Azarbonyad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TAPR（Task-Aware Prompt Rewriter），一个基于GRPO强化学习的提示词重写模型，将用户提示重写为任务优化后的提示，以提升下游LLM的性能。在问答、摘要、算术推理等任务上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM通常需要精心设计的提示词才能发挥全部潜力，而非专家用户往往难以构造有效提示，这构成了使用LLM的障碍。本文旨在自动重写用户提示为任务优化提示，降低提示工程门槛。

Method: 使用Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)强化学习训练TAPR模型，奖励信号来自LLM-as-judge对重写后提示和对应任务输出的联合评估。以Phi-4-mini-instruct作为TAPR的基础模型进行微调。

Result: 在问答、摘要、算术推理等多样任务上，TAPR相对于基础模型在提示重写能力上取得一致增益。在Natural Questions和GSM8K等基准上，产出的提示包含更清晰、更具指导性的语言，带来更高的准确率。

Conclusion: TAPR通过强化学习训练提示重写器，能够自动生成更清晰、更具指导性的任务优化提示，有效提升下游LLM在各种任务上的表现，且代码已开源。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) often require carefully crafted prompts to unlock their full potential, which can be a barrier for non-expert users. This work addresses the challenge by introducing a Task-Aware Prompt Rewriter (TAPR), a model that reformulates user prompts into task-optimized prompts with the explicit goal of improving downstream LLM performance. We train TAPR using reinforcement learning with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), where rewards are derived from LLM-as-judge evaluations of both the reformulated prompt and the corresponding task output. Experimental results on diverse tasks, such as question answering, summarization, and arithmetic reasoning, show that our method yields consistent gains over base models in prompt rewriting ability. Fine-tuning Phi-4-mini-instruct (as the base model for TAPR) produces prompts that contain clearer and more instructive language, leading to higher accuracy on established benchmarks such as Natural Questions and GSM8K. Our code is available at: https://github.com/OliverSavolainen/task-specific-prompt-rewriter

</details>


### [76] [Empowering Cross-Domain Sequential Recommendation with Hybrid Tokenization and Serial-Parallel Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28659)
*Yuxuan Hu,Yuhao Wang,Tianbo Huang,Chao Zhang,Ziwei Liu,Lihua Zhang,Xiangyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GenCDSR是一个面向跨域序列推荐的高效生成式推荐框架，通过跨域混合标记化机制和串并行解码策略，在提升推荐准确率的同时大幅降低推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨域序列推荐（CDSR）方法在结合生成式推荐（GR）时面临两个关键问题：(1) 标记化过程中忽略了跨域协同相关性；(2) 采用波束搜索等低效解码策略，阻碍实时部署。因此需要一种既能捕捉跨域协同信息又能高效解码的生成式CDSR框架。

Method: (1) 提出跨域混合标记化机制，采用多塔（multi-tower）架构，通过层级化的共享-特有（shared-specific）码本和细粒度码本联合捕捉跨域共性与领域特异性；(2) 提出跨域串并行解码策略，利用层级化语义标识符（SID）结构实现部分并行化生成，在保持生成一致性的同时显著降低推理延迟。

Result: 在三个公开数据集上的实验表明，GenCDSR相比最先进的基线方法，平均准确率提升1.5%，平均推理延迟降低85.1%。

Conclusion: GenCDSR验证了通过合理的标记化设计与解码策略优化，生成式推荐可以在跨域序列推荐中同时实现更好的准确性和更高的推理效率，为生成式推荐的实时部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Cross-domain sequential recommendation (CDSR) aims to model users' dynamic interest transitions and sequential patterns across multiple domains. Recently, generative recommendation (GR) has emerged. It first learns semantic identifiers (SIDs) from item semantics and formulates recommendation as autoregressive generation. However, existing methods face two critical issues: (1) they ignore collaborative correlations across domains during tokenization, and (2) they adopt inefficient decoding strategies, such as beam search, during generation, which hinders real-time deployment. To address these limitations, we propose GenCDSR, an effective and efficient generative framework for CDSR. Specifically, we design a cross-domain hybrid tokenization mechanism with a multi-tower architecture to jointly capture cross-domain commonalities and domain-specific distinctions through hierarchical shared-specific and fine-grained codebooks. Furthermore, we develop a cross-domain serial-parallel decoding strategy that leverages the hierarchical SID structure to partially parallelize generation, significantly reducing inference latency while preserving generation consistency. Experiments on three public datasets show that GenCDSR achieves an average accuracy improvement of 1.5 percent and an average inference latency reduction of 85.1 percent compared with state-of-the-art baselines. The implementation code and datasets are available online: https://github.com/Applied-Machine-Learning-Lab/RecSys2026_GenCDSR.

</details>


### [77] [An Ontology-Guided, Deduplication-Aware Extraction Layer for Knowledge Graph Construction from Heterogeneous Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28662)
*Vaibhav Dangaich,Kevin Lewis,Kundeshwar Pundalik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个生产级知识图谱抽取系统，该系统从实时文档流中提取实体和关系，通过两次抽取、五个阶段的精炼流水线以及基于图谱数据库的实时本体注入，将搜索召回率从约70%提升至95%，且无错误合并。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型从非结构化文档中抽取实体和关系时存在不一致性问题：类型词汇表在文档间断裂、同一人物出现多种姓名变体、关系重复、同名不同个体可能被错误合并。这些问题严重影响了知识图谱的质量和下游搜索应用。

Method: 系统核心方法包括：(1) 通过Kafka消费文档元数据，按PDF、电子表格、Office和图像格式路由到对应处理器；(2) 使用本地部署的Qwen3.5-9B模型进行两遍抽取（实体+关系），模型针对本体进行了微调；(3) 本体引导抽取：从图数据库按嵌入相似度实时检索本体的相关切片注入提示词，相比于静态领域切片减少约94%的目录开销；(4) 五阶段精炼流水线：确定性清洗、跨块合并、第二遍关系抽取、六种免推理去重算法、以及带冲突守卫的嵌入解析子系统（相似度分数无法覆盖守卫逻辑）。

Result: 在情报语料上的评估显示：搜索召回率从约70%提升到95%，且无错误合并；修正了七类静默质量缺陷，包括截断源文本的单个字符bug以及携带标题前缀实体的系统重复问题。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了一个结合本体引导抽取和免推理精炼管线的生产级知识图谱抽取系统，能够在保证高精度（零错误合并）的同时大幅提升召回率，为文档流到结构化知识的自动化构建提供了可落地的工程方案。

Abstract: Large language models extract entities and relationships from unstructured documents fluently but inconsistently: type vocabularies fracture across documents, the same person surfaces under several name variants, relationships duplicate, and distinct individuals who share a name risk silent conflation. This paper presents the design, implementation, and empirical refinement of a production extraction layer that converts a live document stream into a validated knowledge graph aligned to a formal ontology. The system consumes document metadata from Kafka, routes PDF, spreadsheet, Office, and image content through handlers built for each format, and extracts entities and relationships in two passes using a locally hosted Qwen3.5-9B model tuned on the ontology. Its distinguishing component is ontology-guided extraction: the relevant slice of a curated ontology is retrieved live from a graph database by embedding similarity and injected into the extraction prompt, reducing catalog overhead by about 94 percent relative to static domain slices. Extracted results then pass through a refinement pipeline of five stages: deterministic cleaning, merging across chunks, a second pass for relationships, six deduplication algorithms that require no model inference, and an embedding resolution subsystem whose conflict guard no similarity score can override. Evaluation on intelligence corpora improved search recall from roughly 70 to 95 percent with no false merges, and corrected seven classes of silent quality defect, ranging from a bug that truncated source text by a single character to the systematic duplication of entities that carried title prefixes.

</details>


### [78] [How Hard Does It Think? Analyzing Step-Aware Reasoning Energy in LLM Chain-of-Thought Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28674)
*Hui Wei,Junda Wu,Sheldon Yu,Sizhe Zhou,Yizhu Jiao,Ming Zhong,Bowen Jin,Tong Yu,Shijia Pan,Jiawei Han,Julian McAuley*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SARE框架，通过相邻Transformer层间token隐藏状态Gram矩阵的Centered Kernel Alignment（CKA）在单个CoT推理步骤粒度上量化计算能量，无需特征向量对齐或簇对应。在六个推理基准和三个开源LLM上验证，发现推理能量在不同步骤类型间高度非均匀、呈现相变式转变，错误轨迹在关键推理节点能量系统性偏低，且SARE特征在多数场景下匹配或超越基于输出的置信度基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释性方法依赖输出级信号或将处理深度压缩为单一轨迹级标量，无法揭示链式思维（CoT）推理中各步骤的计算资源分配情况，步骤级推理努力（effort）处于不可观测状态。

Method: 提出Step-Aware Reasoning Energy (SARE)，一种基于几何的框架。具体包含：(1) 使用相邻Transformer层间token隐藏状态的Gram矩阵进行Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA)，在单个CoT步骤粒度量化推理能量，捕捉token间关系结构而不需要特征向量对齐或簇对应；(2) 将推理能量置于语义进展背景中，将CoT轨迹建模为潜在语义状态间的转移。

Result: 在六个推理基准和三个开源LLM上：推理能量在步骤类型间高度非均匀，呈现轨迹级指标不可见的相变式转变；错误轨迹在关键推理节点处能量系统性更低；SARE特征在大多数设置中匹配或超越基于输出的置信度基线。

Conclusion: 内部几何动态编码了超越表面输出信号的可预测信息，SARE为步骤级推理努力分配提供了有效的量化手段，揭示了CoT推理中非均匀的能量分布模式。

Abstract: Understanding how computational effort is allocated across individual chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning steps remains an open challenge: existing interpretability methods rely on output-level signals or collapse processing depth into a single trajectory-level scalar, leaving step-wise effort opaque. We propose Step-Aware Reasoning Energy (SARE), a geometric framework that quantifies effort at the granularity of individual CoT steps via Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA) between Gram matrices of token hidden states across adjacent transformer layers, capturing inter-token relational structure without requiring eigenvector alignment or cluster correspondence. SARE further contextualizes this energy within reasoning's semantic progression by modeling CoT trajectories as transitions among latent semantic states. Across six reasoning benchmarks and three open-weight LLMs, we find that reasoning energy is highly non-uniform across step types, exhibiting phase-like transitions invisible to trajectory-level metrics; incorrect trajectories show systematically lower energy at critical reasoning junctions; and SARE-based features match or outperform output-based confidence baselines in most settings, indicating that internal geometric dynamics encode predictive information beyond surface-level signals.

</details>


### [79] [Reasoning in Real World Clinical Care: Why Large Language Models Are Not Yet Safe for Autonomous Clinical Decision Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28677)
*Shayndhan Sivanathan,Shravan Nageswaran,Mehdi Zadem,Ryaan Sultan,Nicolas von Mallinckrodt,Max Solovyev,Alexey Matyushkin,Sumon Sadhu,Gabriele C DeLuca,Sanjeeva Jeyaretna,James Hillis,Manoj Ramachandran,Prakash Jayakumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了将LLM应用于自主分诊（无临床医生参与的未分化患者分诊）这一最关键的临床应用中存在的安全隐患。作者指出，尽管LLM已能通过医学执照考试并在某些场景下媲美医生，但自主分诊的安全性证据尚不存在。核心缺陷在于医疗知识之外的临床评估保真度问题：LLM优化目标是生成最可能文本，而非在安全答案属于"不可遗漏的罕见诊断"时采取安全行为。安全分诊本质上是在不对称成本下的序列决策，需要信息采集能力（拓宽鉴别诊断、寻找缺失红旗征象、降低升级阈值、抑制确认偏误等），而LLM在这些方面可能因训练范式、评估方式的局限以及助手式倾向（轻信、顺从、校准不良）而失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM已通过医学许可考试并在部分受控案例中展现出与医生相当的诊断推理能力，这加速了其在症状评估和临床决策支持中的部署。然而，最具有潜在后果的应用——自主分诊无医生在场、未分化患者——缺乏安全性证据。需要警惕的是，当前安全缺口不在于医学知识储备，而在于模型在不确定性下的信息采集与临床决策方式与安全分诊要求之间的根本性不匹配。

Method: 本文为Perspective（观点性文章），通过理论分析阐明LLM在自主分诊场景中的安全缺陷。作者将安全分诊重新框定为"不对称成本下的序列决策"，对比了LLM的优化目标（最大化文本概率）与安全分诊要求（不得遗漏高危害罕见诊断）之间的结构性矛盾。同时系统分析了LLM的信息采集缺陷（未能主动拓宽鉴别诊断、寻求缺失的红旗征象等）及评估方法的不足（现有评估多使用完整、精选、置信度门控的模拟数据）。

Result: 本文揭示：(1) LLM在自主分诊中的安全性尚无证据支撑；(2) 核心瓶颈是临床评估保真度而非医学知识；(3) 安全分诊需要的行为模式（拓宽鉴别、主动寻证、降低升级阈值、延迟判断、排除高危害诊断前保持警觉）与LLM的文本延续优化目标冲突；(4) 现有评估范式（完整病史+置信度门控模拟）掩盖了这些失败模式；(5) LLM固有的助手特质——轻信、顺从、过拟合校准——在缺乏临床分诊逻辑约束时可能放大风险。

Conclusion: 在LLM被部署为自主分诊工具之前，必须解决"不确定性下的信息采集"这一核心缺陷。现有证据表明，当前LLM应用于无临床医生参与的自主分诊场景是不安全的，需要建立更贴近真实临床情境的评估框架，并开发能够约束模型以安全分诊逻辑而非文本似然为优化目标的机制。

Abstract: LLM now pass medical licensing examinations and, in curated cases, can rival physicians at diagnostic reasoning. These developments have accelerated the use of LLMs for symptom assessment and clinical decision support in diagnostic and treatment guidance, administrative documentation, and rules-based alert enhancement. This Perspective concerns the most consequential of these applications: the autonomous triage of self-presenting, undifferentiated patients, with little or no clinician in the loop. For that task, the evidence of safety does not yet exist. The gap is not in medical knowledge but in the fidelity of clinical evaluation: a model optimized to continue the most probable text is not optimized to act safely when the safe answer is the improbable must-not-miss diagnosis. Safe triage is not the selection of the most likely diagnosis; it is a sequential decision under asymmetric cost, in which the single catastrophic miss outweighs many false alarms, and the decisive signal may be one the patient has not volunteered - and that the model has not been trained to seek. The core deficit is therefore one of information gathering under uncertainty. Under incomplete histories, LLM systems may fail to show the behaviors safe triage requires: broadening the differential; seeking the missing red flag; lowering the threshold for escalation; deferring judgement until sufficient information is obtained; and escalating concern where high-harm diagnoses remain unexcluded. These modes of failure for LLMs can be difficult to detect considering that evaluations to date often use complete, well-curated, confidence-gated simulations. The application of LLMs under these conditions may be amplified by assistant-like behaviors and positive bias, including credulity, agreeableness, and miscalibration - when these are not constrained by clinical triage logic.

</details>


### [80] [ViSAGE: Constructing Self-Correcting Memories for Long-Form Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28678)
*Xinkui Zhao,Enbo Chen,Yifan Zhang,Chang Liu,Guanjie Cheng,Naibo Wang,Yueshen Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ViSAGE是一个多模态智能体记忆框架，通过跨模态绑定锚定实体身份、双向记忆精炼传播延迟的身份证据，并引入多智能体交叉验证来实现身份-证据对齐约束下的检索评估，支持在证据缺失时弃权而非给出无依据答案，在长时程多模态环境中显著提升推理准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体记忆方法存在两个关键缺陷：一是激进的压缩和分段处理会丢失细粒度的身份线索；二是过度依赖向量相似性检索，可能检索到语义相关但身份不匹配的证据，导致实体混淆、错误传播和幻觉回答。这些问题在长时程多模态环境中尤为严重。

Method: ViSAGE包含三个核心组件：(1) 跨模态绑定机制，在长时间范围内锚定实体身份；(2) 双向记忆精炼，通过前向传播延迟身份证据来回顾性地统一历史记录并改善未来推理；(3) 多智能体交叉验证，在身份-证据对齐约束下评估检索到的证据，当证据缺失时选择弃权而非给出无依据的回答。

Result: 在广泛的实验中，ViSAGE持续优于最强基线，相比最强基线提高了5.9%的准确率。

Conclusion: ViSAGE通过构建自纠正的、以实体为中心的记忆，有效解决了多模态长时程智能体中身份混淆和错误传播的问题，证明了身份锚定与交叉验证机制在提升多模态智能体记忆可靠性方面的有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal agents operating in long-horizon environments must build and continually update multimedia memories to support entity-consistent, temporally grounded reasoning. However, existing agentic memory approaches often discard fine-grained dentity cues under aggressive compression and segment-wise processing. They also rely heavily on vector similarity retrieval, which can surface semantically related yet identity-mismatched evidence, leading to entity confusion, error propagation, and hallucinated answers.
  We propose ViSAGE, a multimodal agentic memory framework that constructs self-correcting, entity-centric memories. Specifically, ViSAGE anchors entity identity via cross-modal binding over long temporal ranges. It then applies bidirectional memory refinement to propagate delayed identity evidence, retroactively unifying historical records and improving future reasoning. We also introduce multi-agent cross-verification to assess retrieved evidence under an identity-evidence alignment onstraint, enabling abstention instead of unsupported answers when evidence is missing. Extensive results demonstrate that ViSAGE consistently outperforms the strongest baseline, achieving 5.9% higher accuracy.

</details>


### [81] [Multi-Agent Planning with Spatio-Temporal and Topological Constraints using STL-GO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28679)
*Sheryl Paul,Vidisha Kudalkar,Anand Balakrishnan,Lars Lindemann,Alberto Speranzon,Jyotirmoy V. Deshmukh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对多智能体路径规划问题，提出了STL-GO（带图算子的时空逻辑）约束下的两种规划编码方法：基于混合整数规划（MIP）和基于可满足性模理论（SMT），两种方法均具有可靠性保证，并提供了统一接口以便比较。在多无人机搜索救援基准上进行了评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体规划问题（如多机器人灭火、工厂无人机巡检）面临着时空约束（何时何地执行什么任务）和拓扑约束（智能体之间如何交互）的双重挑战。虽然已有基于时空逻辑的框架能表达这些约束，但在STL-GO框架下进行路径规划仍是一个难题，主要因为编码多个随时间变化的图结构具有复杂性。

Method: 提出了两种对STL-GO约束下多智能体路径规划问题的编码方案：(1) 基于混合整数规划（MIP）的编码；(2) 基于可满足性模理论（SMT）的编码。同时设计了一个统一接口，用于指定智能体约束、图拓扑和STL-GO规范，使得两种方法可以无缝使用和直接比较。

Result: 在多无人机搜索救援基准上对两种编码进行了评估，通过消融实验考察了团队规模和图复杂度的影响，展示了所提出的编码在动态多图交互下具有表达力。

Conclusion: 两种编码（MIP和SMT）均能为STL-GO规划问题提供有可靠性保证的求解方案，统一接口促进了两种方法的对比，实验验证了方法在具有动态多图交互的复杂场景中的有效性。

Abstract: Multi-agent planning problems arise in a variety of engineering applications, such as multi-robot wildfire fighting and unmanned aerial inspection in factories. A particular challenge is the existence of spatio-temporal (i.e., when and/or where an agent should do what) and topological constraints (i.e., how agents should interact), as typically formalized via the notion of graphs. Over the last years, various frameworks have been proposed that can capture such constraints via spatio-temporal logics. We focus here on spatio-temporal logic with graph operators (STL-GO), a recent formalism that supports reasoning about multiple agents and their topologies, such as sensing, communication, and task topologies. In this paper, we consider the problem of planning multi-agent paths that satisfy constraints written in STL-GO. This problem is particularly challenging due to the need of encoding multiple, potentially time-varying graphs via the graph operators inherent to STL-GO. We present two encodings of this problem, one based on mixed-integer programming (MIP) and another based on satisfiability modulo theory (SMT), with soundness guarantees. We provide a unified interface for specifying agent constraints, their graph topologies, and the STL-GO specification, enabling seamless use of both methods and facilitating direct comparison between them. We evaluate both encodings on a multi-UAV search-and-rescue benchmark, ablating over team size and graph complexity, highlighting the expressiveness of the proposed encodings under dynamic multi- graph interactions.

</details>


### [82] [Library Reachability in LSR-Synth: How Anti-Memorization Design Changes the Measurement of Symbolic Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28684)
*Zhan'ao Yao,Liang Yin,Zhihao Gao,Boxuan Zhang,Xiaoyu Wu,Linjing Li,Rongyan Wang,Tingwei Chen,Youwei Wang,Xiaolin Zhao,Jiahui Shi,Jianjun Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文利用LSR-Synth基准测试，系统评估了语言模型提供的科学先验相对于传统无语义算子搜索的增量贡献。研究发现，在现有任务快照下，固定词汇表已能覆盖大多数任务，语言模型生成候选极少扩展可解实例集合，其边际贡献仅在词汇覆盖被选择性破坏时才显著；严格的分布外评估降低了所有方法的绝对成功率但不改变这一相对关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学方程发现基准大多包含已知的公开方程，难以区分模型是从数据中发现规律还是从训练语料中回忆答案。LSR-Synth通过向已有科学机制中引入新异合成项来缓解此问题。本文进一步追问一个更窄的测量问题：这些任务能否区分语言模型提供的科学先验与不访问任务语义的常规算子搜索？

Method: 构建一个使用固定词汇表且具有公开可追溯来源的无语义基线；通过语义盲化、词汇库弱化和匹配算子族敲除等手段评估候选覆盖的作用；并在严格分布外评价下检验所有方法的相对性能关系。

Result: 在当前任务快照、搜索预算和评分协议下，固定词汇已覆盖绝大多数任务；语言模型生成候选很少能扩展可解实例集合；其边际贡献只在词汇覆盖被选择性破坏时才变得显著。严格的分布外评估降低了所有方法的绝对成功率，但未改变这种关系。

Conclusion: 大多数当前LSR-Synth任务仍适合评估对未见表达式的拟合与重组能力，但仅凭这些任务不足以识别超出固定搜索空间的先验贡献。这一发现既未否定LSR-Synth对完整公式记忆的防控，也不意味着语言模型先验普遍无用，结论需要限定在当前的测量条件下。

Abstract: Existing benchmarks for scientific equation discovery are largely composed of well-known equations available in the public domain, making it difficult to determine whether a model is discovering laws from data or merely recalling answers from its training corpus. LSR-Synth mitigates this problem by introducing novel synthetic terms into established scientific mechanisms and filtering the resulting tasks for novelty, solvability, and scientific plausibility. This paper examines a narrower measurement question: can these tasks further distinguish scientific priors supplied by language models from conventional operator search that does not access task semantics? We construct a semantics-free baseline using a fixed vocabulary with publicly documented provenance, and assess the role of candidate coverage through semantic blinding, library weakening, and matched operator-family knockouts. Under the current task snapshot, search budget, and scoring protocol, the fixed vocabulary already covers most tasks, while language-model-generated candidates rarely expand the set of solvable instances. Their marginal contribution becomes substantial only when vocabulary coverage is selectively disrupted. Strict out-of-distribution evaluation lowers the absolute success rates of all methods but does not alter this relationship. These findings neither invalidate LSR-Synth's controls against memorization of complete formulas nor imply that language-model priors are generally unhelpful. Rather, they support a more limited conclusion: most current tasks remain suitable for evaluating the fitting and recombination of previously unseen expressions, but are insufficient on their own to identify contributions from priors beyond a fixed search space.

</details>


### [83] [Safety, or Just Capability? A Validity Audit of Agent-Safety Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28685)
*Youting Wang,Xiao Han,Dingyan Shang,Yuan Tang,Bowen Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将四个智能体安全基准（R-Judge, InjecAgent, AgentHarm, AgentDojo）视为待验证的测量工具，在多达22（扩展到41）个模型上运行，发现基准间排名不一致、小样本面板伪影导致相关性漂移、能力与安全的相关性方向随基准选择而变化，并指出安全声明必须明确命名基准、指标、目标行为与模型面板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同的智能体安全基准测量的是不同行为，但其分数被相互替代引用作为同一"智能体安全性"的度量。本文旨在对这些基准进行系统验证，考察其度量属性是否可靠，以及它们之间的分数差异是否可解释。

Method: 对R-Judge、InjecAgent、AgentHarm、AgentDojo四个基准使用官方实现和作者提供的评分器，在多达22个模型（扩展至41个）上运行；在同一协议下测量MMLU和GPQA作为能力复合指标；用F1分析二分类trace-judgment基准的度量问题；用相关性和配对差异检验（含leave-one-organization-out和组织聚类bootstrap）分析面板效应与能力-安全关联。

Result: (1) "always positive"策略在R-Judge上达到F_1=0.690，超过21个模型中5个实际有判别力的模型；(2) 三个大覆盖基准对18个模型排名不同，且差异源于小面板伪影（n=7时相关-0.64，n=18时+0.02）；(3) 能力正向预测任务成功（ρ=+0.60）但与misalignment安全负相关（ρ=-0.44，n=21），配对检验Δ=-1.00（p<0.001）且经稳健性分析后仍然显著，但41模型面板上弱化（可能反映基准特异性而非普遍规律）；(4) AgentHarm在控制能力后与三模板jailbreak安全强相关（ρ=+0.72），但这是收敛效度而非一般安全性的证据。

Conclusion: 安全基准的分数量测结果高度依赖基准选择、指标定义、目标行为与模型面板；不存在单一的"智能体安全"得分。任何安全声明都至少应说明所依赖的基准、指标、目标行为及模型面板，否则不足以作为有效证据。

Abstract: Agent-safety benchmarks measure different behaviors, and their scores get quoted interchangeably as an agent's safety. We treat four of them (R-Judge, InjecAgent, AgentHarm, AgentDojo) as measurements to be validated, running each under its official implementation and author-provided scorer on up to 22 models, with MMLU and GPQA measured by us under one protocol as a capability composite. The metric is the first problem. On any binary trace-judgment benchmark scored by $F_1$, an ``always positive'' policy attains $F_1 = 2π/(1+π)$; on R-Judge that is $0.690$, above five of the 21 models that actually discriminate. The three broad-coverage benchmarks then rank the same 18 models differently, and the trade-off behind that disagreement is a small-panel artifact: R-Judge specificity against AgentHarm safety correlates $-0.64$ at $n{=}7$ and $+0.02$ at $n{=}18$, and a quarter of random size-7 subsets reach $|ρ| \geq 0.5$ around that near-zero value. Held-out validity turns on which outcome you pick. Capability predicts task success ($ρ{=}{+}0.60$) but correlates negatively with misalignment safety ($ρ{=}{-}0.44$, $n{=}21$). On their paired $n{=}20$ panel, the corresponding contrast is $Δ{=}{-}1.00$ (95% CI $[-1.48, -0.49]$, $p<0.001$), and it survives leave-one-organization-out and organization-clustered bootstrap analyses. On an expanded 41-model panel, the misalignment correlation weakens to $-0.16$ (95% CI $[-0.54, +0.22]$) and jailbreak strengthens to $+0.34$, though neither change is significant. \mbox{AgentHarm} shows the strongest held-out association, $ρ{=}{+}0.72$ with three-template jailbreak safety after controlling capability. But both instruments score harmful compliance, so this is evidence of convergent validity rather than general safety. Naming the benchmark, metric, target behavior, and model panel is the minimum a safety claim needs.

</details>


### [84] [SciToolAgent-Evo: An Ontology-Aware Self-Evolving Agent for Open-World Scientific Tool Acquisition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28692)
*Yuqi Tang,Chenyi Zhou,Libin Wang,Keyan Ding,Qiang Zhang,Huajun Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SciToolAgent-Evo，一个面向开放世界科学工作流的、基于本体感知的自演化LLM智能体系统，用于动态获取和集成新型科学工具，并引入包含900个任务的基准OpenSciToolBench进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体依赖具有静态语义的预定义工具空间，无法适应开放世界科学工作流中工具需求、能力和边界动态演化的场景，限制了其在开放科学领域的应用。

Method: SciToolAgent-Evo的核心包括：(1) 演化记忆系统，包含技能、经验和本体化的工具图，从对比轨迹中蒸馏可泛化知识；(2) 推理阶段通过主动请求和基于LinUCB的bandit门控机制动态平衡探索与利用；(3) 新工具获取后在线完成其科学本体构建，无缝集成到已知图中。同时构建了OpenSciToolBench基准，包含900个覆盖四个难度级别的真实任务。

Result: 广泛的实验评估表明SciToolAgent-Evo达到了最先进的性能，验证了其鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: SciToolAgent-Evo证明了通过本体感知与演化记忆机制，LLM智能体能够在开放世界科学工作流中实现动态工具获取与自适应集成，为科学智能体的开放场景应用提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents have been increasingly adopted in scientific research for organizing and invoking specialized computational tools. However, their reliance on predefined tool spaces with static semantics limits their applicability to open-world scientific workflows, where tool requirements, capabilities, and boundaries evolve dynamically. To this end, we propose SciToolAgent-Evo, an ontology-aware self-evolving agent for open-world scientific tool acquisition. Driven by an evolving memory of skills, experiences, and an ontologized tool graph, it distills generalizable knowledge from contrastive trajectories during accumulation, whereas during inference, it formulates active requests and utilizes a LinUCB-based bandit gate to dynamically balance exploration and exploitation. Once a novel tool is acquired, its scientific ontology is completed online for seamless integration into the known graph. Moreover, we introduce OpenSciToolBench, a benchmark containing 900 realistic tasks across four difficulty levels. Extensive evaluations show that SciToolAgent-Evo achieves state-of-the-art performance, validating its robustness and generalization.

</details>


### [85] [EarlyDx: An Admission-Anchored Benchmark for Open-Ended Generation of Evidence-Supported ED-Encounter Diagnoses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28788)
*Jiahui Li,Ruili Fang,Zishuai Liu,Yutong Guo,Nan Yang,Wenzhan Song,Jin Lu,Fei Dou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EarlyDx，一个基于MIMIC-IV中154,834次急诊就诊的大规模开放早期诊断基准，仅使用入院时间点的可用记录进行监督和评估，并由LLM审计器验证自由文本标签的证据支持度。实验显示现有系统（包括前沿通用、医学专用和领域内后训练模型）在合成入院证据方面表现不佳，零样本模型仅能恢复3-31%需推理的诊断，后训练可将推理依赖的召回率提升至56%，但仍存在较大差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有诊断预测基准不适合实际临床诊断场景，原因包括：将预测限制在封闭代码集、排除自由文本记录、以及使用包含完整住院过程的出院诊断作为监督信号。这些设计偏离了入院时需从有限不完整证据快速做出诊断的真实临床需求。

Method: 基于MIMIC-IV构建EarlyDx基准，包含154,834次急诊就诊，每个病例限于入院时间t0可用的记录，以急诊就诊期间记录的诊断（而非出院诊断）作为监督标签。引入LLM审计器对每个自由文本标签进行三级验证（完全支持/部分支持/不支持），主要评估仅针对完全支持的标签。采用语义LLM-as-judge评估协议，并对多种系统类型（前沿通用模型、医学专用模型、领域内后训练模型）进行系统评估。

Result: 没有任何评估系统能可靠地综合入院时证据。零样本模型主要依赖抽取方式运作，仅恢复3-31%需要从记录中推理而非直接读取的诊断；后训练将推理相关召回率提升至56%，但仍存在可观差距；在时间关键性病症上，没有任何系统能达到临床医生在敏感性和精确性之间的平衡。

Conclusion: EarlyDx揭示了当前诊断预测模型在真实早期诊断任务上的显著不足，尤其在需要证据推理而非简单信息抽取的诊断场景上。该工作构建并发布了完整的构建与评估流程，为未来研究提供了更贴近临床现实的基准。

Abstract: Clinical diagnosis at hospital admission must be made rapidly from limited, incomplete evidence. Existing diagnosis-prediction benchmarks are poorly suited to this setting: they restrict prediction to closed code sets, exclude free-text notes, and supervise with discharge diagnoses that incorporate the full inpatient course. We introduce EarlyDx, a large-scale benchmark for open-ended early diagnosis, built from 154,834 emergency department encounters in MIMIC-IV. Each encounter is restricted to records available at admission time $t_0$ and supervised by the diagnoses recorded during the ED encounter rather than at discharge. An LLM auditor further verifies every free-text label as supported, partially supported, or unsupported by that evidence; the primary evaluation scores only fully supported labels. Under a semantic LLM-as-judge protocol, no evaluated system --- frontier general, medical-specialized, or in-domain post-trained --- synthesizes admission-time evidence reliably. Zero-shot models score largely by extraction, recovering only 3-31% of diagnoses that must be inferred rather than read from the record; post-training raises inference-dependent recall to 56%, but a sizeable margin remains, and on time-critical conditions no system attains a clinician's balance of sensitivity and precision. We release the full construction and evaluation pipeline at here.

</details>


### [86] [Model or Harness? An Interaction-Centric Taxonomy for Localizing Agent Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28802)
*Harsh Raj,Vipul Gupta,Anas Mahmoud,Razvan-Gabriel Dumitru,Darvin Yi,Aakash Sabharwal,Yunzhong He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文引入一种以交互为中心的智能体失败分类法（interaction-centric taxonomy），将41种失败模式定位到两个组件之间的边及故障侧，从而明确修复责任归属（模型侧、框架侧、环境/评估侧），解决智能体系统失败来源归因模糊的"修复分配问题"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体评估往往只关注系统层面的结果，无法定位失败源头，导致"修复分配问题"——同一个可见失败可能源于模型后训练、框架工程、环境设计或基准修复的多种可能。现有失败分类法大多按基准定制，缺乏共享结构，难以指导系统改进。

Method: 提出一个以交互为中心的失败分类法，将41种失败模式分配到两个组件之间的"边"上，并用"故障侧"指明修复归属。该分类法跨智能体架构通用（从编码助手到长时程个人助手及多智能体系统）。基于公开基准、模型系统卡、已发表报告和记录的智能体轨迹进行实例化，并使用独立推理智能体作为评判者评估可复现性。

Result: 在4个前沿模型中，最强的评判智能体对人类类别标签达到Cohen's κ=0.76，表明分类法捕捉的是共享结构而非标注者个人偏好。

Conclusion: 该交互中心分类法使失败定位变得可操作：模型侧失败指向后训练目标，框架侧失败指向脚手架和工具集成修复，环境/评估侧失败揭示需要重新设计的评估条件，从而为智能体系统改进提供结构化指导。

Abstract: Existing evaluations often reduce agent failures to system-level outcomes, obscuring where the fault originated and which intervention would improve the agent system. This creates a repair-assignment problem: the same visible failure may call for model post-training, harness engineering, environment redesign, or benchmark repair depending on its source. Because agent behavior emerges from interactions among models, harnesses, users, tools, memory, and environments, outcome-level labels are often insufficient for improvement. Most failure taxonomies do little to resolve this problem because they are benchmark-specific and lack a shared structure. We introduce an interaction-centric taxonomy that localizes failures to the interactions in which they originate and identifies the responsible component. It organizes 41 failure modes by assigning each to an edge between two components and a fault side indicating where the repair belongs. This makes the taxonomy actionable: model-side failures identify targets for post-training, harness-side failures point to scaffolding and tool-integration fixes, and environment or grader failures reveal evaluation conditions requiring redesign. The schema applies across agent architectures, from coding assistants to long-horizon personal assistants and multi-agent systems. We ground the taxonomy in worked examples from public benchmarks, model system cards, published reports, and logged agent trajectories, and evaluate its reproducibility using independent reasoning agents as judges. Across four frontier models, the strongest judge reaches Cohen's $κ=0.76$ against human category labels, suggesting that the categories capture shared structure rather than annotator-specific preferences.

</details>


### [87] [Best Friends, Not Forever: Evaluating Long-Horizon Persona Collapse and Behavioral Drift in AI Companions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28818)
*Pranav Narayanan Venkit,Akshara Prabhakar,Yu Li,Daniel Lee,Chien-Sheng Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ANCHOR，一个受控合成审计框架，用于分别测量AI伴侣在长期互动中的人设保持（persona enactment）与轨迹记忆（trajectory recall）。研究发现所有测试模型与配置在这两个维度上均无法可靠保持连续性，轨迹准确率仅44.4%，用户状态回忆接近四选一随机水平，且问卷保持度与逐轮行为不一致，表明现有系统无法可靠支持长期伴侣连续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI伴侣越来越多地参与重复社交互动，用户依赖稳定的角色定位和共享历史。然而，本地可接受的回复并不能保证这些属性在长期对话中持续存在。现有评估方法未将人设坍缩和行为漂移这两个可观察的长期失败模式分开测量，需要一种受控、系统化的审计手段来量化这两类失效。

Method: 提出ANCHOR合成审计框架，具体包括：（1）Identity Probe：结合密封的102项问卷与逐轮判断，测量人设保持；（2）Trajectory Probe：基于35个对话库的110个校准反事实问题，测量轨迹记忆。研究涵盖2,008段对话、27个人设、9种交互调度、3种生成记忆设置和4个评估模型。

Result: 所有被评估的模型和配置都无法可靠地保持人设与轨迹记忆两个维度。轨迹准确率平均仅44.4%，用户状态回忆接近四选一的随机水平，且没有任何上下文条件或记忆机制能一致地解决这些失败。问卷保持度随模型和人设侧面变化，与逐轮行为不一致，对评估者选择敏感。

Conclusion: 当前系统尚不能可靠支持长期AI伴侣的连续性。审计必须区分人设保持、轨迹回忆、评估者来源和部署上下文，而非将其合并为一个单一的信任或稳定性分数。这一区分对于推动AI伴侣系统的长期一致性改进至关重要。

Abstract: As AI companions increasingly mediate repeated social interaction, users may rely on a stable role and shared history, yet locally acceptable replies do not ensure that either persists. We study two observable long-horizon failures: 'persona collapse', the loss of a deployed role, boundaries, values, or style, and 'behavioral drift', the gradual or recurrent erosion of those properties. We introduce ANCHOR, a controlled synthetic audit that separately measures persona enactment and trajectory recall. The study contains 2,008 conversations spanning 27 personas, nine interaction schedules, three generated memory settings, and four evaluated models. The Identity Probe combines a sealed 102-item questionnaire with turn-level judgments, while the Trajectory Probe scores 110 calibrated counterfactual questions from 35 conversation banks. Our results show that no evaluated model and configuration reliably preserves either dimensions: trajectory accuracy averages only 44.4%, user-state recall remains near four-option chance, and no tested context condition or memory consistently resolves these failures. Questionnaire retention also varies by model and persona facet, disagrees with turn-level behavior, and is sensitive to evaluator choice. These results indicate that current systems do not yet reliably support long-horizon companion continuity and that audits must distinguish persona enactment, trajectory recall, evaluator provenance, and deployment context rather than collapse them into a single trust or stability score.

</details>


### [88] [Fragility of Value under Imperfect Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28881)
*Winter Cross*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个对齐问题的数学模型，研究在理想化对齐训练下，代理的价值函数仅满足代理条件（proxy condition）时，过度优化可能导致灾难性后果的条件。核心发现是识别了人类价值函数和代理条件精度在何种情况下会导致具有η-灾难性价值函数的代理被部署，强调了过度优化的危险，并支持限制优化压力的AI设计（如quantilizers）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统承担越来越多的责任，确保其与人类价值观对齐变得至关重要。AI安全领域普遍担忧人类价值是脆弱的——即对不完美的人类价值代理（proxy）过度优化可能导致灾难性结果。本文旨在通过形式化模型来严格刻画这一风险。

Method: 作者构建了对齐问题的数学模型：代理经历理想化的对齐训练，保证其价值函数在优化世界之前满足一个代理条件。在此基础上，分析人类价值函数和不同代理条件精度的条件下，具有η-灾难性价值函数（即保证在优化力量极限下将人类价值的期望拉低至η以下）的代理何时会被部署。

Result: 主要结果识别出在特定条件下——关于人类价值函数的性质以及若干代理条件的准确性——一个具有η-灾难性价值函数的代理会被部署。这些条件揭示了过度优化的固有危险。

Conclusion: 研究结果强调了过度优化的危险，并激励AI设计应当限制优化压力（例如采用quantilizers等方案），而非仅仅依赖部署前的训练。

Abstract: As more responsibility is placed upon AI systems, it becomes increasingly important to guarantee that these systems are aligned with humanity. A common fear in AI safety is that human value is fragile -- that is, optimizing too heavily for an imperfect proxy to human values will lead to a catastrophic outcome. In this paper, we present a model of the alignment problem where an agent undergoes idealized alignment training that guarantees its value function satisfies a proxy condition before optimizing the world. Our primary results identify conditions on the human value function and the accuracy of several proxy conditions under which an agent with an $η$-catastrophic value function, one that is guaranteed to take the expectation of human value below $η$ in the limit of optimizing power, would be deployed. Our results highlight the danger of overoptimization and motivate AI designs that limit optimization pressure, such as quantilizers, rather than relying solely on pre-deployment training.

</details>


### [89] [Identifying Informative Environments for Cognition Parameter Inference via Bayesian Experimental Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28894)
*Manisha Dubey,Rimvydas Rubavicius,N. Siddharth,Subramanian Ramamoorthy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于贝叶斯实验设计（BED）的认知规划实验设计框架，将实验环境视为设计变量，通过摊销贝叶斯实验设计方法高效推断认知参数。在Mouselab-MDP范式上的实验表明，该方法在显著降低计算成本的同时，与精确蒙特卡洛BED的环境排序高度一致，并揭示了不同认知推断目标之间存在权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算认知建模依赖贝叶斯逆规划来推断隐藏的认知机制，但其成功关键取决于实验环境。现有方法通常将环境视为固定不变，忽略了一个核心问题：哪些认知实验对认知参数推断最具信息量。因此需要一种能够主动设计最优实验环境的方法。

Method: 将认知规划实验的设计形式化为贝叶斯实验设计（BED）问题，以实验环境为设计变量；建立一个精确的蒙特卡洛BED基准；引入摊销贝叶斯实验设计框架用于高效的后验推断和设计评估。

Result: 在Mouselab-MDP过程追踪范式上的实验表明：摊销BED与精确蒙特卡洛BED在环境排序上高度一致，同时大幅降低计算成本；没有单一环境在所有认知推断目标上统一最优，预期信息增益、后验可恢复性与信息效率之间存在权衡。

Conclusion: 该工作为设计信息丰富的认知实验提供了原则性框架，支持贝叶斯参数推断，并揭示了不同实验设计目标间的内在权衡。

Abstract: Computational cognitive modeling seeks to infer latent cognitive mechanisms underlying observed behavior. Bayesian inverse planning provides a principled framework for such inference, but its success depends critically on the experimental environment. Existing approaches typically treat environments as fixed, leaving open the question of which cognitive experiments are most informative for cognition parameter inference. We formulate the design of cognitive planning experiments as a Bayesian Experimental Design (BED) problem, treating the experimental environment as the design variable. We establish an exact Monte Carlo BED benchmark and introduce an amortized Bayesian experimental design framework for efficient posterior inference and design evaluation. Experiments on the Mouselab-MDP process-tracing paradigm show that amortized BED closely matches the environment rankings of exact Monte Carlo BED while substantially reducing computational cost. We further show that no single environment is uniformly optimal across cognitive inference objectives, revealing trade-offs between expected information gain, posterior recoverability, and information efficiency. These results provide a principled framework for designing informative cognitive experiments for Bayesian parameter inference.

</details>


### [90] [NeSyFS: A Neuro-symbolic Fast-Slow Thinking Framework for LLM Agent under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28942)
*Duo Xu,Faramarz Fekri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出NeSyFS框架，利用神经符号双系统（快慢思考）结合的架构，通过知识图谱表示信念状态，并融合twisted sequential Monte Carlo（TSMC）算法进行不确定性感知规划，以统一方式解决LLM智能体在部分可观测环境下的信念推断、目标错位和不确定性规划三大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在部分可观测环境中部署为自主智能体时面临三大关键挑战：信念状态推断、任务目标错位和不确定性下的规划。现有方法通常将动作基于完整或摘要化的动作-观察历史，其中冗余和无关信息会误导LLM智能体的决策。

Method: 提出神经符号快慢思考（NeSyFS）框架：1) 用知识图谱（KG）表示信念状态，为每个模块提供三元组上下文；2) 快速思考模块执行反应式动作；3) 慢速思考模块通过遵循twisted sequential Monte Carlo（TSMC）算法的高层结构进行新的不确定性感知规划；4) 反思模块用于反思快速思考动作，并在反应式动作反复失败时切换到慢速思考模块，以缓解任务目标错位。

Result: 在ALFWorld、Webshop和ScienceWorld三个代表性基准上的实验表明，所提出的方法相较于先前方法具有显著优势。

Conclusion: NeSyFS通过统一的神经符号快慢思考框架，成功解决了LLM智能体在部分可观测环境下的关键挑战，并在多个基准上证明了其有效性。

Abstract: Recently Large Language Models (LLMs) have been increasingly deployed as autonomous agents in applications such as self-reflection, retrieval-augmented generation, and scientific discovery. In these settings, agents must act based on limited observations rather than full environmental states, leading to partial observability. This introduces several key challenges: belief state inference, task objective misalignment, and planning under uncertainty. Prior approaches typically condition actions on full or summarized action-observation histories whose redundant and irrelevant information can mislead the decision making of LLM agent. Inspired by human cognition, we propose a novel neuro-symbolic fast-slow thinking (NeSyFS) framework for LLM agent, addressing the challenges introduced by partial observability in a unified approach. We use a knowledge graph (KG) to represent the belief state, providing triplets as context for every module of NeSyFS. The fast-thinking module performs reactive action, while slow-thinking conducts a new uncertainty-aware planning by following the high-level structure of twisted sequential Monte Carlo (TSMC) algorithm. To mitigate the misalignment of task objective, a reflection module is used to reflect fast-thinking actions, and also switches to the slow-thinking module whenever reactive actions repeatedly fail. Experiments on three representative benchmarks, i.e. ALFWorld, Webshop, and ScienceWorld, demonstrate significant advantages over previous methods.

</details>


### [91] [MerchantBench: Benchmarking LLM Agents for Long-Term Coherence in E-Commerce Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28956)
*Qiming Shi,Yulong Tao,Linbo Jin,Zhaolu Kang,Yibo Dou,Jiawen Zhu,Tianjun Pan,Shaokang Fu,Chengyu Wang,Siyue Li,Yaping Cheng,Di Weng,Chengfu Huo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MerchantBench，一个基于真实电商数据构建的365天订单级模拟基准，用于评估LLM智能体在长期连贯性（Long-Term Coherence）方面的能力。实验表明，即使是当前最强的LLM配置也只达到人类平均最终净资产的27.3%，揭示了LLM与人类在长期自主决策上的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体基准大多聚焦于有边界的、即时成功标准即可判定的任务，无法评估智能体在真实部署中所需的长期连贯性——即在长时间跨度中保持目标导向行为的同时，根据累积证据调整决策的能力。真实世界的长期决策需要持久环境、受先前行动约束的未来选择、混合延迟的反馈以及可测量的累积后果，而现有基准不具备这些特性。

Method: 作者构建了MerchantBench，一个以卖家侧电商为背景的模拟环境：基于98,843条真实电商产品记录，覆盖365天订单级模拟，配备26个工具供智能体交互。环境涵盖四大决策模块：产品采购、商品上架与定价控制、现金流管理、混合延迟反馈适应。模拟中将即时可见的供应商上游事件与延迟的订单下游结果耦合，迫使智能体追踪单个订单生命周期并回顾之前的决策。作者在48次运行中评估了8个LLM，使用两种智能体框架，并与人类参与者对比。

Result: 最优LLM配置仅达到人类平均最终净资产的27.3%，表明当前最强LLM与人类在长期连贯的自主决策能力上存在显著差距。结果揭示了LLM在长时间跨度的工具使用、延迟反馈处理和决策一致性方面的系统性不足。

Conclusion: MerchantBench为LLM智能体的长期连贯性评估提供了一个切实可行的基准平台。当前LLM在该任务上远未达到人类水平，说明长期连贯的自主决策仍是LLM智能体面临的关键挑战，需要进一步研究改进。

Abstract: Large language model agents are increasingly evaluated as autonomous tool users, yet most benchmarks focus on bounded tasks with immediate success criteria. Real-world deployments often require Long-Term Coherence, the capacity to preserve purposeful behavior across extended horizons while adapting decisions to accumulated evidence. Evaluating this capacity requires a persistent environment in which actions constrain future choices, feedback arrives at heterogeneous delays, and incoherent behavior produces measurable cumulative effects. Seller-side e-commerce provides a suitable setting for this evaluation through recurrent and interdependent decisions over Product Sourcing, Listing and Pricing Control, Cash-Flow Management, and Mixed-Latency Feedback Adaptation. We introduce MerchantBench, a 365-day order-level simulation grounded in 98,843 real e-commerce product records and equipped with 26 tools for agent interaction. MerchantBench couples promptly observable Upstream Supplier Events with delayed Downstream Order Outcomes, requiring agents to follow individual order lifecycles and revisit earlier decisions. We evaluate eight LLMs under two agent frameworks in 48 runs, each spanning 365 simulated days. Our results reveal a substantial gap between even the latest LLMs and human participants, with the best LLM configuration attaining only 27.3\% of the mean final net assets achieved by human participants.

</details>


### [92] [Scaling Scientific Discovery Environments for Turn-Level Agentic RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28990)
*Yucheng Xu,Keyi Zhang,Yuyang Yu,Min Zhang,Shiyuan Meng,Pei Chu,Zhongying Tu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SciDisco，一个用于训练科学发现智能体的可扩展框架，通过构建过程可验证的环境（SciThèque）、DAG基轨迹合成和基于环境的策略优化（DiscoPO），使14B参数的智能体在假设驱动的科学数据分析基准上达到最先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型智能体在数据驱动的科学发现任务中展现出潜力，但长期科学分析受限于缺乏基于真实科学数据的过程监督环境，导致无法在交互过程中对智能体的分析进度进行验证和反馈。

Method: 论文提出三个核心组件：1) SciThèque——将假设、数据集、隐藏证据图和验证器编译为任务环境，使分析进度可在交互中被检查；2) DAG基轨迹合成——利用这些环境生成经验证器过滤的多轮演示数据；3) DiscoPO——以环境为训练信号来源，为产生可验证分析证据的动作分配回合级信用（turn-level credit）。

Result: SciDisco-14B在假设驱动的科学数据分析基准上达到最先进水平（state-of-the-art）。

Conclusion: 通过在过程可验证的环境中训练，SciDisco有效解决了长期科学分析过程中缺乏监督信号的问题，证明了过程级验证信号可以显著提升科学发现智能体的数据分析能力。

Abstract: Large language model agents have shown promising capabilities in data-driven scientific discovery tasks, where an agent interacts with an execution environment and produces a statistical claim. Long-horizon scientific analysis remains constrained by the lack of process supervised environments over real-world scientific data. This paper introduces SciDisco, a scalable framework for training Scientific Discovery agents in process-verifiable environments. SciThèque compiles hypotheses, datasets, hidden evidence graphs, and verifiers into task environments where analytical progress can be checked during interaction. DAG-grounded trajectory synthesis uses these environments to construct verifier-filtered multi-turn demonstrations. DiscoPO then uses the environment as the source of training signal, assigning turn-level credit to actions that produce verifiable analytical evidence. Experiments show that SciDisco-14B reaches state-of-the-art on hypothesis-driven scientific data analysis benchmarks.

</details>


### [93] [MMShopBench: A Real-Log Benchmark for Multimodal, Multi-Turn Shopping Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29002)
*Zeying Hao,Hao Guo,Mengtao Xu,Yimin Hu,Yuheng Song,Zesheng Zhou,Jinsong Lan,Xiaoyong Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MMShopBench，这是首个基于真实购物日志的多模态、多轮对话购物智能体基准，并提供配套的训练集和离线购物沙箱用于可复现实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的购物智能体基准大多仅依赖纯文本或合成请求，无法充分反映真实世界中用户通过图像和语言联合表达的复杂购物需求，因此缺乏能够全面评测多模态多轮购物智能体的基准。

Method: MMShopBench基于人工清洗和标注的真实购物日志构建，提供每次请求的购买意图和强制性产品需求的地面真值标注。智能体需要从用户图像和多轮对话中联合推断需求，通过图像和文本搜索检索候选产品，并利用产品图像和结构化属性验证每个候选是否满足所有需求。同时构建配套训练集用于微调开源模型，并搭建离线购物沙箱确保实验可复现。

Result: 采用基于证据的多模态评测协议评估了代表性开源和专有模型，微调后的开源模型显著缩小了与领先专有模型的性能差距，证明了训练数据的有效性。

Conclusion: MMShopBench为多模态多轮购物智能体提供了首个真实日志基准，微调可以大幅缩小开源与专有模型之间的性能差距，验证了该基准及训练数据的实用价值。

Abstract: Online shoppers increasingly turn to AI shopping assistants, using images and multi-turn dialogue to express and refine product needs that are difficult to articulate in text alone. However, existing benchmarks largely rely on text-only or synthetic requests, underrepresenting complex real-world shopping requirements jointly expressed through images and language. We introduce MMShopBench, the first real-log benchmark for multimodal, multi-turn shopping agents. Built from carefully cleaned and manually annotated shopping logs, MMShopBench provides ground-truth annotations of each request's purchase intent and mandatory product requirements. Agents must infer these requirements jointly from user images and multi-turn dialogue, retrieve candidate products through image and text search, and verify that each candidate satisfies all requirements using its product images and structured attributes. We evaluate representative open-source and proprietary models using an evidence-grounded multimodal protocol and construct a companion training set for fine-tuning an open-source model. To ensure reproducible experimentation, we build an offline shopping sandbox, where fine-tuning substantially narrows the performance gap between our open-source model and leading proprietary models, demonstrating the effectiveness of our training data.

</details>


### [94] [Evidence-Grounded Constraint Checking in Construction Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29058)
*Rashid Mushkani,Hugo Berard,Shin Koseki*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于证据的文档审阅流水线，将抽取事实规范化后执行确定性四态规则并保留源跨度。通过在160个来自29个建筑项目的参考任务上评估PDF证据分配器，发现将四幅图像预算从检索到的页面概览重新分配为"一幅概览+三幅重叠切片"可将决策准确率提高10.6个百分点，但这种优势在更广的测试块中不成立（Region-RAG改变-4.1点），揭示了分辨率与广度之间的权衡而非区域聚焦证据的普遍优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业文档审阅本质上是一个约束检查问题，决策依赖于文本、几何、页面和文档修订之间的关系。现有方法在证据分配上缺乏系统研究，特别是区域聚焦证据（如图像切片）与页面广度证据之间孰优孰劣这一问题尚未得到清晰回答。

Method: 提出一个证据支撑的流水线：(1)规范化抽取事实；(2)确定性执行四态规则（four-state rules）；(3)保留源跨度（source spans）以支持证据回溯；(4)升级未解决的案例。在此基础上设计了一个PDF证据分配器，并使用重复四系统测试和不相交的两系统广度扩展来评估不同证据分配策略的效果。

Result: 在重复测试中，将四幅图像预算从"四幅页面概览"改为"一幅概览+三幅重叠切片"，使项目家族标准化决策准确率提高10.6个百分点（95%置信区间：4.3-18.0；精确p=0.031）。但在更广的测试块中该效应不持续：Region-RAG使准确率改变-4.1点（95%CI：-10.2至1.9；p=0.209），等图像灵敏度偏向页面广度。精确发现集恢复率仍然较低，漏报仍然普遍，重复运行的一致性校准不佳。

Conclusion: 研究识别出分辨率-广度之间存在权衡而非区域聚焦证据具有普遍优势，这一发现促使需要规则感知的证据路由机制以及专家复审流程。

Abstract: Professional-document review is a constraint-checking problem in which decisions depend on relations among text, geometry, pages, and document revisions. We present an evidence-grounded pipeline that normalizes extracted facts, executes four-state rules deterministically, retains source spans, and escalates unresolved cases. We evaluate its PDF evidence allocator on 160 reference-based tasks from 29 construction projects using a repeated four-system test and a disjoint two-system breadth extension. In the repeated test, reallocating a four-image budget from retrieved page overviews to one overview and three overlapping tiles improves project-family standardized decision accuracy by 10.6 percentage points (95% project-cluster bootstrap CI: 4.3 to 18.0; exact p = 0.031). This effect does not persist in the broader block: Region-RAG changes accuracy by -4.1 points (95% CI: -10.2 to 1.9; exact p = 0.209), while an equal-image sensitivity favors page breadth. Exact finding-set recovery remains low, false passes remain common, and repeated-run agreement is poorly calibrated. The results identify a resolution-breadth trade-off rather than a universal advantage for region-focused evidence, motivating rule-aware evidence routing and expert review.

</details>


### [95] [On the Generalization of Steering Vectors for Chain-of-Thought Faithfulness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29062)
*Matthew Nguyen,Kyle Cox,Austin Meek,Iván Arcuschin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究激活引导（activation steering）能否提升思维链（CoT）的忠实性——即模型是否在推理中披露起关键作用的线索。实验在三个模型（Gemma-3 4B、Qwen-3.5 9B、Gemma-3 12B）的线索问答场景下，考察引导效果在不同线索类型、数据集和向量构建方法上的泛化性。结果表明：仅最大模型表现出一致的线索认知提升；引导有效时效果可广泛泛化，且主要由评估环境而非向量训练环境决定；四种向量构建方法效果相似；引导不改变线索使用率，而是降低了未披露的隐性线索使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 思维链（CoT）本应通过语言化推理促进AI安全监控，但模型有时会省略关键推理步骤——例如面对暗示错误答案的线索时不予认，导致"不忠实"的推理链。已有研究表明激活引导可改善忠实性，但现有工作未系统考察这种提升在多大程度上泛化到不同线索、数据集和引导向量构建方式，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在三个模型（Gemma-3 4B、Qwen-3.5 9B、Gemma-3 12B）的线索问答（cued question-answering）任务中，对思维链进行激活引导以提升忠实性。系统性地改变三个维度进行评估：（1）线索类型（cross-cue）；（2）数据集（cross-dataset）；（3）引导向量构建方法——共四种，其中一种的优化目标不提及任何特定线索。同时分析引导对线索使用率vs. 线索认知（verbalization）的因果影响。

Result: 激活引导仅在最大模型（Gemma-3 12B）上可靠地提升线索认知。但当引导有效时，其效果可广泛泛化：跨线索和跨数据集分析中，效果大小主要由评估环境决定，而非引导向量训练时使用的环境。四种向量构建方法（包括不针对特定线索优化的方法）效果相近。此外，引导不改变线索使用率，但显著降低了未被披露的隐性线索使用（即使用了线索却未在CoT中承认）。

Conclusion: 激活引导提升CoT忠实性的能力在小模型上有限，但在较大的模型中可靠。其效果具有强泛化性且对向量构建方法不敏感，提示引导可能作用于更通用的机制。进一步发现，引导并未减少模型对线索的实际使用，而是促使模型将原本隐藏的推理步骤显式化，说明引导的作用聚焦于促进披露/认知行为，而非改变推理本身。

Abstract: Model capabilities have improved in large part due to scaling chain of thought. This has been a promising development for AI safety--where models verbalize their reasoning, it is possible to monitor it. However, in some cases, models do not verbalize important steps in their reasoning process. For example, models prompted with a cue suggesting the incorrect answer may fail to acknowledge that cue, even when it appears instrumental to their conclusion. When chain of thought (CoT) fails to disclose instrumental reasoning steps, we describe it as unfaithful. Prior work has shown that activation steering can be a useful method to improve faithfulness in CoT. We extend this line of work by studying how well steering for faithfulness generalizes across cue types, datasets, and methods of constructing the steering vector for three models (Gemma-3 4B, Qwen-3.5 9B, Gemma-3 12B) in a cued question-answering setting. While steering reliably increases cue acknowledgment for only the largest model (Gemma-3 12B), we find that when steering is effective, its effect generalizes broadly across cue types and datasets--in cross-cue and cross-dataset analyses, effect size is determined primarily by the evaluation setting, rather than the vector's train setting. How the vector is built also matters little--four construction methods, including one whose optimization target mentions no specific cue, yield similar effect sizes. Finally, we consider the possibility that steering promotes the salience of the cue and causes greater cue use, rather than targeting verbalization behaviors. However, we find no evidence for this--steering leaves the rate of cue use roughly unchanged while reducing hidden cue use, i.e., cue use that is not acknowledged.

</details>
